[{"id":5955,"slug":"style-customizer","link":"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/style-customizer\/","title":{"rendered":"Style Customizer"},"content":{"rendered":"<p>The Style Customizer lets you change how your form looks \u2014 colors, fonts, spacing, borders, and button style \u2014 without writing any CSS. It&#8217;s built directly into the form builder&#8217;s <strong>Style<\/strong> tab, with a live preview that updates as you make changes.<\/p>\n<p>This article covers the current Style Customizer. If you styled a form before this update, don&#8217;t worry \u2014 your existing look carries over automatically the first time you open the Style tab. See <strong>Migrating an Older Form<\/strong> below.<\/p>\n<h2>Where to Find It<\/h2>\n<ol>\n<li>Go to <strong>Everest Forms \u2192 All Forms<\/strong> and edit the form you want to style.<\/li>\n<li>Click the <strong>Style<\/strong> tab at the top of the builder.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n<figure><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/08\/style-customizer-all-forms-menu-v3-1.png\" alt=\"Everest Forms admin sidebar with the All Forms menu item highlighted\" \/><\/figure>\n<figure><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/08\/style-customizer-style-tab-v3.png\" alt=\"Everest Forms builder tab bar with the Style tab highlighted\" \/><\/figure>\n<h2>The Style Tab Layout<\/h2>\n<p>The Style tab is split into two panes:<\/p>\n<ul>\n<li><strong>Left \u2014 Design controls.<\/strong> A single scrollable list: <strong>Pre-defined<\/strong> (Template and Colors) at the top, then <strong>Elements<\/strong> (Form, Text, Inputs, Choices, Button, Messages) to fine-tune each part, then <strong>Advanced<\/strong> (Custom CSS) at the bottom.<\/li>\n<li><strong>Right \u2014 Live preview.<\/strong> Shows exactly how the form will look, updating instantly as you adjust a control. Use the <strong>Desktop \/ Tablet \/ Mobile<\/strong> switch above the preview to check how your styling holds up on smaller screens.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n<figure><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/08\/style-customizer-design-panel-layout-v3.png\" alt=\"The Style tab's two-pane layout, design controls on the left and live preview on the right\" \/><\/figure>\n<blockquote><p><strong>Note:<\/strong> Nothing is saved until you click <strong>Save<\/strong> in the top-right corner. Switching to another tab (e.g. Fields) while you have unsaved style changes will ask you to confirm before leaving.<\/p><\/blockquote>\n<h2>Color Palette<\/h2>\n<p>At the top of the Design list, click <strong>Colors<\/strong> to open the palette screen. It has two parts:<\/p>\n<ul>\n<li><strong>Your Palette<\/strong> \u2014 the six colors (background, text, borders, button, and so on) your form is actually using right now, shown as a swatch strip. On Pro, click the pencil icon to edit any of the six directly \u2014 the live preview updates as you type. On Free, this strip is view-only.<\/li>\n<li><strong>Presets<\/strong> \u2014 a grid of ready-made color sets below it. Hover a card to preview it live on the form without committing to anything; click to apply it. Applying a preset always shows up as one step in <strong>Undo<\/strong>, so it&#8217;s safe to try a few before settling on one.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n<figure><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/08\/style-customizer-color-palette-v3.png\" alt=\"Colors screen showing Your Palette and the preset color grid\" \/><\/figure>\n<blockquote><p><strong>Note (Free plan):<\/strong> Two presets \u2014 <strong>Classic<\/strong> and <strong>Monochrome<\/strong> \u2014 are available on the Free plan. All other presets shown are Pro; selecting one will prompt you to upgrade. Editing Your Palette&#8217;s colors directly is also a Pro feature. See Free vs Pro below.<\/p><\/blockquote>\n<p><strong>If this form was styled before this update:<\/strong> whatever custom palette it used to have shows up right at the front of the Presets grid, marked with a purple <strong>Custom<\/strong> badge \u2014 it isn&#8217;t hidden or lost. Click it to keep using it exactly as before. If you no longer need it, Pro plans can remove it with the delete icon on the card. What you <em>can&#8217;t<\/em> do anymore is rename it or edit its colors in place \u2014 that &#8220;create\/edit a saved palette&#8221; workflow has been retired. To move it forward instead: apply it (or it may already be applied, showing as your current Your Palette), then use the <strong>Your Palette<\/strong> editor above (Pro) to adjust any of its six colors directly \u2014 you&#8217;re editing your form&#8217;s live colors at that point, not the old saved entry, so there&#8217;s nothing to &#8220;fix&#8221; beyond that.<\/p>\n<h2>Design Elements<\/h2>\n<p>Below Pre-defined, click any of the six elements to open its settings:<\/p>\n<h3>Form<\/h3>\n<p>Background color and image, corner rounding, overall width, and the form&#8217;s font family.<\/p>\n<h3>Text<\/h3>\n<p>Typography (size, weight, color, style) for <strong>Label<\/strong>, <strong>Sublabel<\/strong>, <strong>Description<\/strong>, and <strong>Section Title<\/strong> text \u2014 switch between them using the tabs at the top of the panel.<\/p>\n<h3>Inputs<\/h3>\n<p>Styling for text boxes, dropdowns, textareas, date pickers, and the file upload area, with separate <strong>Normal<\/strong> and <strong>Focus<\/strong> states.<\/p>\n<h3>Choices<\/h3>\n<p>One shared style for every &#8220;pick a choice&#8221; field \u2014 radio buttons, checkboxes, Yes\/No, image choices, star\/scale ratings, Likert rows, and payment pickers (including the Subscription Plan card). Includes:<\/p>\n<ul>\n<li><strong>Selected color<\/strong>, <strong>mark size<\/strong>, <strong>unselected border<\/strong><\/li>\n<li><strong>Style variation<\/strong> \u2014 Default, Outline, or Filled<\/li>\n<li><strong>Option font size<\/strong>, <strong>text color<\/strong>, and <strong>font style<\/strong><\/li>\n<li><strong>Alignment<\/strong> \u2014 left, center, or right<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n<h3>Button<\/h3>\n<p>Styling for the Submit button (and Multi-Part navigation\/upload buttons, which follow it), with separate <strong>Normal<\/strong> and <strong>Hover<\/strong> states.<\/p>\n<h3>Messages<\/h3>\n<p>Styling for the success, error, and validation messages shown to visitors after they submit the form. <em>(Pro)<\/em><\/p>\n<h2>Click-to-Edit in the Live Preview<\/h2>\n<p>Instead of hunting through the panels, click directly on any part of the form in the live preview \u2014 a label, a button, an input, a choice field \u2014 and the Design panel automatically navigates to that element&#8217;s specific settings, with the element itself outlined in the preview so you always know what you&#8217;re editing.<\/p>\n<figure><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/08\/style-customizer-click-to-edit-live-preview-v3.png\" alt=\"Clicking the Name field in the live preview opens its matching Inputs settings panel\" \/><\/figure>\n<blockquote><p><strong>Tip:<\/strong> Hover over an element in the preview first; it highlights so you know exactly what you&#8217;re about to click before you commit.<\/p><\/blockquote>\n<h2>Apply Theme Style<\/h2>\n<p>Toggle <strong>Apply Theme Style<\/strong> (below Pre-defined) to let your active WordPress theme style the form instead of Everest Forms&#8217; own default look. Your color, spacing, and font choices above still apply on top of either.<\/p>\n<h2>Undo, Redo, and Reset<\/h2>\n<ul>\n<li>Use the <strong>Undo<\/strong>\/<strong>Redo<\/strong> buttons above the design panels to step back and forward through your recent changes.<\/li>\n<li><strong>Reset this section<\/strong> (top of each panel) clears just that element back to its defaults.<\/li>\n<li><strong>Reset all styles<\/strong> (top of the Design list) reverts the entire form to its unstyled default look.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n<h2>Templates<\/h2>\n<p>Click <strong>Template<\/strong> at the top of the Design list to browse ready-made looks and apply one in a single click. It has the same two-part layout as Colors:<\/p>\n<ul>\n<li><strong>Your Template<\/strong> \u2014 a live thumbnail matching your form&#8217;s current styling exactly, right now, however you arrived at it (a preset, hand-picked colors, or a mix).<\/li>\n<li><strong>Presets<\/strong> \u2014 the template grid below it. Hover a card to preview it live on the form; click to apply. Two presets, <strong>Clearline<\/strong> and <strong>Ledger<\/strong>, are available on the Free plan; the rest (Carbon Mono, Lavendar, Scribble, Lines, Sage Card, Editorial, Midnight Rain) are Pro.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n<figure><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/08\/style-customizer-templates-v3.png\" alt=\"Templates screen showing Your Template and the preset grid\" \/><\/figure>\n<blockquote><p><strong>Note:<\/strong> Applying a template replaces your current colors, fonts, and spacing with the template&#8217;s own \u2014 it&#8217;s a starting point you can still fine-tune afterward.<\/p><\/blockquote>\n<p><strong>If this form was styled before this update:<\/strong> any custom template you&#8217;d saved for it shows up first in the Presets grid, marked <strong>Custom<\/strong> \u2014 same treatment as a custom palette (see above). Click it to reapply, or Pro plans can delete it with the card&#8217;s delete icon. It can no longer be renamed or edited as a saved entry \u2014 to build on it, apply it and then adjust the result with the Design Elements panels (Form, Text, Inputs, Choices, Button, Messages) below; those changes apply to your form directly and show up live in Your Template above.<\/p>\n<h2>Custom CSS<\/h2>\n<p>Click <strong>Custom CSS<\/strong> under Advanced to write your own CSS. This is applied site-wide \u2014 the same Additional CSS used across every form on your site, not just this one \u2014 so a rule you add here will show up wherever it applies, on any form.<\/p>\n<figure><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/08\/style-customizer-custom-css-v3.png\" alt=\"Custom CSS screen with an example rule and quick-insert selector chips\" \/><\/figure>\n<h2>Free vs Pro<\/h2>\n<table>\n<thead>\n<tr>\n<th><\/th>\n<th>Free<\/th>\n<th>Pro<\/th>\n<\/tr>\n<\/thead>\n<tbody>\n<tr>\n<td>Color palette<\/td>\n<td>2 presets (Classic, Monochrome); view-only Your Palette<\/td>\n<td>2 + 9 additional presets; edit Your Palette&#8217;s colors directly<\/td>\n<\/tr>\n<tr>\n<td>Form \/ Text \/ Inputs \/ Choices \/ Button design controls<\/td>\n<td>\u2014<\/td>\n<td>\u2714<\/td>\n<\/tr>\n<tr>\n<td>Messages (success\/error\/validation) styling<\/td>\n<td>\u2014<\/td>\n<td>\u2714<\/td>\n<\/tr>\n<tr>\n<td>Templates<\/td>\n<td>2 presets (Clearline, Ledger)<\/td>\n<td>2 + 7 additional presets<\/td>\n<\/tr>\n<tr>\n<td>Custom CSS<\/td>\n<td>\u2714<\/td>\n<td>\u2714<\/td>\n<\/tr>\n<\/tbody>\n<\/table>\n<p>On the Free plan, styling a form means picking one of the two built-in palette or template presets (and\/or writing Custom CSS) \u2014 the granular design panels are visible so you can see what&#8217;s possible, but are locked until you upgrade.<\/p>\n<h2>Migrating an Older Form<\/h2>\n<p>If a form was styled using the classic (older) Style Customizer, opening its Style tab for the first time automatically converts that styling to the current format \u2014 nothing is lost, including any Pro styling if your Pro license has since lapsed and later comes padding: 0px;\">na<\/span>vigate to&nbsp;<strong>Plugins &gt; Add Plugin &gt; Upload Plugi<\/strong>n,&nbsp;select the downloaded zip file, and then install and activate the plugin and addon simultaneously.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Now, you&#8217;re ready to use this add-on on your site.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Alternative Method:<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Once you have activated the Everest Forms Pro license on your site, go to <strong>Everest Forms &gt; Add-ons<\/strong>.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Search for the &#8216;<strong>Frontend Listing<\/strong>&#8216; add-on.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Toggle to activate the addon.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_MIt86uouVG.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"486\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_MIt86uouVG-1024x486.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5735\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_MIt86uouVG-1024x486.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_MIt86uouVG-300x142.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_MIt86uouVG-768x364.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_MIt86uouVG-1536x729.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_MIt86uouVG-360x171.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_MIt86uouVG.png 1920w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-code\"><code>For a detailed guide, read our documentation on <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/premium-feature-unlock\/\">installing and activating Everest Forms Premium and its features<\/a>. <\/code><\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Setup and Configuration<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Once you have finished installing the add-on, you will have to create frontend listing\/s as per your requirements. To do this, go to <strong>Everest Forms &gt; Frontend Listing &gt; Add New.<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Creating a List<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"486\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_v0VIdaKNBJ-1024x486.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5736\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_v0VIdaKNBJ-1024x486.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_v0VIdaKNBJ-300x142.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_v0VIdaKNBJ-768x364.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_v0VIdaKNBJ-1536x729.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_v0VIdaKNBJ-360x171.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_v0VIdaKNBJ.png 1920w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><figcaption class=\"wp-element-caption\">Frontend Listings &#8211; Main Listing Page<\/figcaption><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">After clicking on the &#8216;<strong>Add New&#8217; <\/strong>button, you will be able to create a new frontend listing. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"449\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss3-new-listing-editor-1024x449.png\" alt=\"Frontend Listing Settings\" class=\"wp-image-5720\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss3-new-listing-editor-1024x449.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss3-new-listing-editor-300x132.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss3-new-listing-editor-768x337.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss3-new-listing-editor-360x158.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss3-new-listing-editor.png 1536w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><figcaption class=\"wp-element-caption\">Frontend Listing &#8211; Add New Page<\/figcaption><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">General Settings<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Configure basic display and access options.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Choose form to display entries: <\/strong>Choose the form whose entries you want to display.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Allow Access to Guest:<\/strong> Enable to let non-logged-in users view the list.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Allow Export from list:<\/strong> Enable to let users download entries as CSV.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Choose Listing Color:<\/strong> Pick a color scheme to match your website\u2019s design.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1324\" height=\"310\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss4-general-settings.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5721\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss4-general-settings.png 1324w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss4-general-settings-300x70.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss4-general-settings-1024x240.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss4-general-settings-768x180.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss4-general-settings-360x84.png 360w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1324px) 100vw, 1324px\" \/><figcaption class=\"wp-element-caption\">Frontend Listing &#8211; General Settings<\/figcaption><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Filter Settings<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Control what content is shown and how it\u2019s filtered from here.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Display Search Form:<\/strong> Enable this option to show or hide the search box for the frontend listing.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Fields to be displayed in table:<\/strong> Select which form fields (e.g., Name, Email) appear in the entry list.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Fields to be displayed in detail page:<\/strong> Choose fields to be displayed when users click \u201cView Details.\u201d<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Hide View Details: If<\/strong> you don\u2019t want visitors to see the details, you can leave this option unchecked.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>View Details in popup:<\/strong> If you\u2019re allowing visitors to see the details of your list, you can choose to show the details in the pop-up. The details will be shown on a new page if this option is unchecked.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Edit Details:<\/strong> Enable&nbsp;Edit Details&nbsp;to let users modify entries directly from the frontend.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1310\" height=\"406\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss5-filter-settings.png\" alt=\"Filter Settings\" class=\"wp-image-5722\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss5-filter-settings.png 1310w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss5-filter-settings-300x93.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss5-filter-settings-1024x317.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss5-filter-settings-768x238.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss5-filter-settings-360x112.png 360w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1310px) 100vw, 1310px\" \/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Pagination and Results Settings<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">From here, you can manage how entries are paginated and displayed<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Display Amount Filter:<\/strong> Check to let visitors choose how many entries they want to view (e.g., 10, 25, 50).<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Default number of entries per page:<\/strong> Set the default number of entries shown (e.g., 10, 20).<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Number of entries per page:<\/strong> You can set how many entries you want to show on a page out of the total entries from here.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Info Text:<\/strong> Enter your message here to display the number of entries out of the total.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Access denied text:<\/strong> For restricted access, you can add custom texts to show when access is denied (e.g.,&nbsp;\u201cYou must log in to view entries\u201d).<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Search box placeholder text:<\/strong> Edit the search bar\u2019s placeholder text (e.g.,&nbsp;\u201cSearch entries&#8230;&#8221;)<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1310\" height=\"404\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss6-pagination-settings.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5723\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss6-pagination-settings.png 1310w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss6-pagination-settings-300x93.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss6-pagination-settings-1024x316.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss6-pagination-settings-768x237.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss6-pagination-settings-360x111.png 360w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1310px) 100vw, 1310px\" \/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Adding the List on a Page or Post<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Once all the configuration has been finished, you can copy the shortcode from the \u2018<strong>Shortcode<\/strong>\u2019 meta box. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_9yGHTAhZ6Y.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"486\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_9yGHTAhZ6Y-1024x486.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5743\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_9yGHTAhZ6Y-1024x486.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_9yGHTAhZ6Y-300x142.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_9yGHTAhZ6Y-768x364.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_9yGHTAhZ6Y-1536x729.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_9yGHTAhZ6Y-360x171.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_9yGHTAhZ6Y.png 1920w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Next, add it to the page\/post where you want to display the frontend lists. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/add-frontend-shortcode-to-page.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"341\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/add-frontend-shortcode-to-page-1024x341.png\" alt=\"Add Frontend Shortcode to Page\" class=\"wp-image-5268\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/add-frontend-shortcode-to-page-1024x341.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/add-frontend-shortcode-to-page-300x100.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/add-frontend-shortcode-to-page-768x256.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/add-frontend-shortcode-to-page-360x120.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/add-frontend-shortcode-to-page.png 1070w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Click <span style=\"margin: 0px; padding: 0px;\"><strong>&#8220;Update&#8221;<\/strong>&nbsp;or&nbsp;<strong>&#8220;Publish&#8221;<\/strong><\/span> to save the page. Once your form has the entries, you can view the list on the frontend of your website. Visit the page\/post on your website\u2019s front end. The list will display entries based on your configuration.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Frontend View:<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_uFSnSBp9Fm.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"749\" height=\"444\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_uFSnSBp9Fm.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5737\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_uFSnSBp9Fm.png 749w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_uFSnSBp9Fm-300x178.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_uFSnSBp9Fm-360x213.png 360w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 749px) 100vw, 749px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Frontend List Interactions<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Users can view, export, edit, and manage entries if permissions are enabled.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Export List<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">To export the list, users can click on the<strong> Export <\/strong>button, which downloads the file in CSV format.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_5EKC4viwTE.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"535\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_5EKC4viwTE-1024x535.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5738\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_5EKC4viwTE-1024x535.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_5EKC4viwTE-300x157.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_5EKC4viwTE-768x401.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_5EKC4viwTE-360x188.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_5EKC4viwTE.png 1321w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Detail View<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">For the detailed view option, uncheck <strong>Hide View Details<\/strong>. You can check this option if you don&#8217;t want users to see the details.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Next, you can check <strong>View Details in the popup <\/strong>to show the details. It will display all fields selected for display.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_SKeSrWO16k.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"188\" https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_SKeSrWO16k-300x55.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_SKeSrWO16k-768x141.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_SKeSrWO16k-1536x282.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_SKeSrWO16k-360x66.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_SKeSrWO16k.png 1753w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Here&#8217;s an example of a Popup view for details: <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss13-detail-popup.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"450\" https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss13-detail-popup-360x158.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss13-detail-popup.png 1535w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Edit Entries<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">To let users edit the details, ensure&nbsp;<strong>Edit Details<\/strong>&nbsp;is enabled in your Frontend Listing settings (<em>Filter Settings &gt; Check <strong>Edit Details<\/strong><\/em>).<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_w4UDn5n9OT.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1699\" 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_w4UDn5n9OT-1536x363.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_w4UDn5n9OT-360x85.png 360w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1699px) 100vw, 1699px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">On the frontend listing, click the&nbsp;<strong>Edit<\/strong>&nbsp;button in the &#8220;Edit&#8221; column, and users will be redirected to the original form, pre-filled with the entry\u2019s data.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_5dYTcWR7fL.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"429\" https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_5dYTcWR7fL-360x151.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_5dYTcWR7fL.png 1158w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Modify the entry and click&nbsp;<strong>Submit<\/strong>. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss15-edit-form.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1535\" height=\"730\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss15-edit-form.png 1535w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss15-edit-form-300x143.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss15-edit-form-1024x487.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss15-edit-form-768x365.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss15-edit-form-360x171.png 360w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1535px) 100vw, 1535px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You\u2019ll return to the frontend list, and the changes will be reflected immediately.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss16-changes-reflected.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"450\" https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss16-changes-reflected-360x158.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss16-changes-reflected.png 1535w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Similarly, if the form contains files, images, or signatures, editing to replace them is also possible from both the frontend listing and the admin.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/edit-uploaded-files-for-frontend-listing.gif\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1423\" height=\"671\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/07\/edit-uploaded-files-for-frontend-listing.gif\" alt=\"Edit Uploaded Files for Frontend Listing\" class=\"wp-image-5269\"\/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">As an admin, you can edit the files through <strong>Everest Forms &gt; Entries<\/strong>. Click on the <strong>View <\/strong>under the <strong>Action <\/strong>column of the entry you want to edit. Next, click on the <strong>Edit <\/strong>button to replace the file or any entries. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_mvank2LD1a.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"527\" needs.<\/p>\n","protected":false},"doc_category":[56]},{"id":5714,"slug":"everest-forms-wp-abilities-api-integration","link":"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/everest-forms-wp-abilities-api-integration\/","title":{"rendered":"Everest Forms WP Abilities API Integration"},"content":{"rendered":"\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Everest Forms registers its core operations as <strong>abilities<\/strong> through the WordPress Abilities API, making them available to AI agents, automation tools, and external integrations \u2014 including Claude Desktop and custom LLM agents via the MCP protocol.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Each ability maps to a specific Everest Forms operation and enforces the same permission model as the plugin&#8217;s REST API \u2014 only users with the correct WordPress capability can execute it.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Prerequisites<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>WordPress 6.0+<\/strong> and <strong>PHP 7.4+<\/strong><\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Everest Forms<\/strong> (free version) installed and activated \u2014 version 3.4.0 or later<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>A WordPress user with <strong>Administrator<\/strong> role or a role that has Everest Forms capabilities assigned<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>An <strong>Application Password<\/strong> for the user \u2014 generated from <strong>WordPress Admin \u2192 Users \u2192 Profile \u2192 Application Passwords<\/strong><\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>An MCP-compatible client \u2014 Claude Desktop, a custom agent, or any HTTP client that speaks JSON-RPC 2.0<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Setup: Connect Claude Desktop to Your Site<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Step 1 \u2014 Install the MCP Adapter plugin<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Download the latest release ZIP from the <a href=\"https:\/\/github.com\/WordPress\/mcp-adapter\/releases\/tag\/v0.5.0\" data-type=\"link\" data-id=\"https:\/\/github.com\/WordPress\/mcp-adapter\/releases\/tag\/v0.5.0\" rel=\"nofollow noopener\" target=\"_blank\">WordPress\/mcp-adapter GitHub releases page<\/a>.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Go to WordPress Admin \u2192 Plugins \u2192 Add New \u2192 Upload Plugin and install the ZIP.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Activate the plugin.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Step 2 \u2014 Generate an Application Password<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Go to <strong>WordPress Admin \u2192 Users \u2192 Profile<\/strong>.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Scroll down to <strong>Application Passwords<\/strong>.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Enter a name (e.g. <em>Claude Desktop<\/em>) and click <strong>Add New Application Password<\/strong>.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Copy the generated password \u2014 you will not see it again.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Step 3 \u2014 Add the MCP server to Claude Desktop<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Open the Claude Desktop configuration file:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>macOS:<\/strong> <code>~\/Library\/Application Support\/Claude\/claude_desktop_config.json<\/code><\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Windows:<\/strong> <code>%APPDATA%Claudeclaude_desktop_config.json<\/code><\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Add the following entry:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-code\"><code>{\n  \"mcpServers\": {\n    \"everestforms-site\": {\n      \"command\": \"npx\",\n      \"args\": &#91;\"-y\", \"@automattic\/mcp-wordpress-remote\"],\n      \"env\": {\n        \"WP_API_URL\": \"https:\/\/yoursite.com\/wp-json\/mcp\/mcp-adapter-default-server\",\n        \"WP_API_USERNAME\": \"your-admin-username\",\n        \"WP_API_PASSWORD\": \"xxxx xxxx xxxx xxxx xxxx xxxx\"\n      }\n    }\n  }\n}<\/code><\/pre>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><em>Replace\u00a0<\/em><code>yoursite.com<\/code><em>, the username, and the Application Password with your own values<\/em><\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Step 3 \u2014 Restart Claude Desktop<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">After saving the config, restart Claude Desktop. The Everest Forms abilities will be discovered automatically and available as tools in every conversation.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Using Everest Forms Abilities in Claude Desktop<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Once connected, you can manage your forms and entries in plain English. Claude Desktop resolves your intent to the matching ability and executes it with the correct parameters \u2014 no manual API calls needed.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Example prompts:<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><em>List all my published forms.<\/em><\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><em>Create a contact form with name, email, phone, and a message field.<\/em><\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><em>How many unread entries does the Job Application form have?<\/em><\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><em>Mark all entries from last week as read.<\/em><\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><em>Duplicate the Newsletter Signup form and rename it to Newsletter Signup \u2014 Summer 2026.<\/em><\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><em>Show me the analytics summary across all forms.<\/em><\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Available Abilities<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">All abilities are registered under the <code>everest-forms<\/code> namespace. The MCP endpoint exposes <strong>22 abilities<\/strong> across five categories.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Forms<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-table\"><table class=\"has-fixed-layout\"><thead><tr><th>Ability<\/th><th>Description<\/th><\/tr><\/thead><tbody><tr><td><code>list-forms<\/code><\/td><td>List all forms with id, title, and status. Accepts <code>limit<\/code> (default 50) and <code>status<\/code> filter.<\/td><\/tr><tr><td><code>get-form<\/code><\/td><td>Get a single form including its fields, settings, and layout structure.<\/td><\/tr><tr><td><code>create-form<\/code><\/td><td>Create a new form from a descriptor. New forms are created inactive by default \u2014 pass <code>status: \"publish\"<\/code> to make them live immediately.<\/td><\/tr><tr><td><code>update-form<\/code><\/td><td>Update an existing form&#8217;s title, fields, layout, or settings. Supports <code>dry_run<\/code> for preview without saving.<\/td><\/tr><tr><td><code>delete-form<\/code><\/td><td>Permanently delete a form by ID.<\/td><\/tr><tr><td><code>duplicate-form<\/code><\/td><td>Duplicate an existing form and return the new form ID.<\/td><\/tr><tr><td><code>update-form-status<\/code><\/td><td>Change a form&#8217;s status to <code>publish<\/code>, <code>draft<\/code>, or <code>trash<\/code>.<\/td><\/tr><tr><td><code>list-field-types<\/code><\/td><td>List all field types registered on the site, including which addon each type requires.<\/td><\/tr><\/tbody><\/table><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Entries<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-table\"><table class=\"has-fixed-layout\"><thead><tr><th>Ability<\/th><th>Description<\/th><\/tr><\/thead><tbody><tr><td><code>list-entries<\/code><\/td><td>List entries for a form. Accepts <code>limit<\/code>, <code>offset<\/code>, and <code>status<\/code> filter (<code>read<\/code>, <code>unread<\/code>, <code>starred<\/code>).<\/td><\/tr><tr><td><code>get-entry<\/code><\/td><td>Get the full field data for a single entry by ID.<\/td><\/tr><tr><td><code>create-entry<\/code><\/td><td>Programmatically submit an entry for a form.<\/td><\/tr><tr><td><code>update-entry-fields<\/code><\/td><td>Patch field values on an existing entry.<\/td><\/tr><tr><td><code>update-entry-status<\/code><\/td><td>Change an entry&#8217;s status to <code>read<\/code>, <code>unread<\/code>, <code>starred<\/code>, or <code>trash<\/code>.<\/td><\/tr><tr><td><code>delete-entry<\/code><\/td><td>Delete a single entry. Pass <code>permanent: true<\/code> for a hard delete.<\/td><\/tr><tr><td><code>bulk-delete-entries<\/code><\/td><td>Delete multiple entries by ID array. Pass <code>permanent: true<\/code> for hard delete.<\/td><\/tr><tr><td><code>set-entry-starred<\/code><\/td><td>Star or unstar an entry.<\/td><\/tr><tr><td><code>set-entry-viewed<\/code><\/td><td>Mark an entry as read or unread.<\/td><\/tr><tr><td><code>count-entries<\/code><\/td><td>Return the total entry count for a form, optionally filtered by status.<\/td><\/tr><\/tbody><\/table><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Addons<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-table\"><table class=\"has-fixed-layout\"><thead><tr><th>Ability<\/th><th>Description<\/th><\/tr><\/thead><tbody><tr><td><code>list-addons<\/code><\/td><td>List all installed Everest Forms addons with their active status and EVF feature toggle state.<\/td><\/tr><tr><td><code>describe-field-type<\/code><\/td><td>Return the full JSON schema for a field type, including supported properties and required addon.<\/td><\/tr><tr><td><code>activate-addon<\/code><\/td><td>Activate an installed Everest Forms addon by plugin file path. Handles both WP activation and the EVF feature toggle.<\/td><\/tr><\/tbody><\/table><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Analytics<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-table\"><table class=\"has-fixed-layout\"><thead><tr><th>Ability<\/th><th>Description<\/th><\/tr><\/thead><tbody><tr><td><code>analytics-summary<\/code><\/td><td>Return a high-level snapshot \u2014 total forms, total entries, entries per form, and entries over a date range.<\/td><\/tr><\/tbody><\/table><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Permissions<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Each ability enforces the same capability checks as the Everest Forms REST API:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Viewing forms and entries requires <code>everest_forms_view_forms<\/code> or <code>everest_forms_view_entries<\/code><\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Creating or editing forms requires <code>everest_forms_create_forms<\/code> or <code>everest_forms_edit_forms<\/code><\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Deleting forms or entries requires <code>everest_forms_delete_forms<\/code> or <code>everest_forms_delete_entries<\/code><\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Activating addons requires <code>activate_plugins<\/code> \u2014 Administrator level<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Unauthenticated requests are rejected with HTTP <code>401<\/code>. Users without the required capability receive a <code>403<\/code> for that specific ability while still being able to call abilities they do have access to.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Tools that modify site state (create, update, delete operations) carry MCP <code>destructiveHint: true<\/code> annotations. Claude Desktop reads these and shows a confirmation prompt before invoking any destructive ability.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Advanced: create-form Descriptor<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The <code>create-form<\/code> and <code>update-form<\/code> abilities accept a rich descriptor beyond a simple field list.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Flat fields array<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The simplest approach \u2014 one field per row:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-code\"><code>{\n  \"title\": \"Contact Form\",\n  \"status\": \"publish\",\n  \"fields\": &#91;\n    { \"type\": \"first-name\", \"label\": \"First Name\", \"required\": true },\n    { \"type\": \"email\",      \"label\": \"Email\",       \"required\": true },\n    { \"type\": \"textarea\",   \"label\": \"Message\" }\n  ]\n}<\/code><\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Multi-column layout<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Use the <code>layout<\/code> array and assign <code>grid<\/code> positions to place multiple fields side by side:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-code\"><code>{\n  \"title\": \"Registration Form\",\n  \"layout\": &#91;\n    {\n      \"row\": &#91;\n        { \"type\": \"first-name\", \"label\": \"First Name\", \"grid\": 1 },\n        { \"type\": \"last-name\",  \"label\": \"Last Name\",  \"grid\": 2 }\n      ]\n    },\n    { \"row\": &#91;{ \"type\": \"email\", \"label\": \"Email\" }] }\n  ]\n}<\/code><\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Multi-part (step) forms<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Requires the <strong>Multi-Part Forms<\/strong> addon. Define steps in <code>multi_part.parts<\/code> and assign each row to a step with <code>part: N<\/code>:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-code\"><code>{\n  \"title\": \"Job Application\",\n  \"multi_part\": { \"parts\": &#91;{ \"name\": \"Personal Info\" }, { \"name\": \"Experience\" }] },\n  \"layout\": &#91;\n    { \"row\": &#91;{ \"type\": \"first-name\", \"label\": \"First Name\" }], \"part\": 1 },\n    { \"row\": &#91;{ \"type\": \"textarea\",   \"label\": \"Previous Role\" }],  \"part\": 2 }\n  ]\n}<\/code><\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Dry-run mode<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Pass <code>\"dry_run\": true<\/code> to validate the descriptor and preview the resulting field list without writing anything to the database.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Addon-gating<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">If a field type requires an inactive addon, the endpoint returns a structured error naming the addon and whether to install or activate it:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-code\"><code>{\n  \"code\": \"unregistered_type\",\n  \"message\": \"Field type \"likert\" requires the Survey, Polls and Quiz addon.\",\n  \"data\": {\n    \"type\": \"likert\",\n    \"addon\": \"Survey, Polls and Quiz\",\n    \"plugin\": \"everest-forms-survey-polls-quiz\/everest-forms-survey-polls-quiz.php\",\n    \"action\": \"activate\"\n  }\n}<\/code><\/pre>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Call <code>activate-addon<\/code> with the returned <code>plugin<\/code> value, then retry the original request.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">The MCP Endpoint Directly<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can also call the endpoint from any HTTP client without Claude Desktop. The transport is JSON-RPC 2.0 over HTTP POST.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-code\"><code># Sanity check\ncurl -s https:\/\/yoursite.com\/wp-json\/everest-forms\/v1\/mcp   -u \"admin:your_application_password\"\n\n# Call an ability\ncurl -s -X POST https:\/\/yoursite.com\/wp-json\/everest-forms\/v1\/mcp   -u \"admin:your_application_password\"   -H \"Content-Type: application\/json\"   -d '{\n    \"jsonrpc\": \"2.0\",\n    \"id\": 1,\n    \"method\": \"tools\/call\",\n    \"params\": { \"name\": \"list-forms\", \"arguments\": { \"status\": \"publish\" } }\n  }'<\/code><\/pre>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Supported JSON-RPC methods: <code>initialize<\/code>, <code>tools\/list<\/code>, <code>tools\/call<\/code>, <code>ping<\/code>. The endpoint also supports <strong>batch requests<\/strong> \u2014 send an array of request objects to execute multiple calls in a single round-trip.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">WordPress Abilities API Integration<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">When the <a href=\"https:\/\/github.com\/WordPress\/abilities-api\" rel=\"nofollow noopener\" target=\"_blank\">WordPress Abilities API<\/a> plugin is installed, Everest Forms additionally registers all 22 abilities into the WP Abilities registry under the <code>everest-forms<\/code> category. This exposes them to any Abilities-aware plugin or MCP adapter through the standard <code>\/wp-json\/wp\/v2\/abilities<\/code> REST surface \u2014 no additional configuration required.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">If the Abilities API is not installed, Everest Forms&#8217; own MCP endpoint works independently. An admin notice appears on Everest Forms screens with a link to install the API.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Frequently Asked Questions<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Can I restrict which abilities a user can access?<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Yes. Each ability enforces the corresponding Everest Forms capability. Use a plugin like <strong>User Role Editor<\/strong> to assign or remove capabilities per role to scope access precisely.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Are new AI-created forms live immediately?<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">No. Forms created via <code>create-form<\/code> are <strong>inactive<\/strong> by default and do not collect submissions until explicitly published. Pass <code>\"status\": \"publish\"<\/code> to make a form live on creation.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Does the endpoint require HTTPS?<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Not technically, but it is strongly recommended. Application Passwords transmitted over plain HTTP expose credentials. Always use HTTPS in production.<\/p>\n","protected":false},"doc_category":[26]},{"id":5689,"slug":"how-to-create-a-form-with-ai","link":"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/how-to-create-a-form-with-ai\/","title":{"rendered":"How to Create a Form with AI?"},"content":{"rendered":"\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Everest Forms comes with a built-in AI form builder that lets you describe the form you need in plain English and instantly generates it \u2014 no manual field dragging required. class=\"wp-element-caption\">The Add New Form screen showing both creation options.<\/figcaption><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Creating Your Form with AI<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Step 1: Describe Your Form<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">On the AI form builder screen, you&#8217;ll see a prompt input area at the center of the page. Type a plain-English description of the form you need.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image aligncenter size-full\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1536\" height=\"674\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss-prompt-blank.png\" alt=\"Create with AI screen \u2013 What should we build today prompt input\" class=\"wp-image-5706\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss-prompt-blank.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss-prompt-blank-300x132.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss-prompt-blank-1024x449.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss-prompt-blank-768x337.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss-prompt-blank-360x158.png 360w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1536px) 100vw, 1536px\" \/><figcaption class=\"wp-element-caption\">The Create with AI screen \u2013 describe your form in plain words.<\/figcaption><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Example prompts:<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><em>&#8220;Create a contact form with name, email, phone, and a message field.&#8221;<\/em><\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><em>&#8220;I need a job application form with personal details, work experience, and a file upload for CV.&#8221;<\/em><\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><em>&#8220;Build a customer feedback survey with rating fields and a comment box.&#8221;<\/em><\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can be as specific or as general as you like. The AI will infer the appropriate field types, labels, and layout from your description.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image aligncenter size-full\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1536\" height=\"674\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss-prompt-typed.png\" alt=\"AI prompt with a contact form description typed and Generate button active\" class=\"wp-image-5707\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss-prompt-typed.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss-prompt-typed-300x132.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss-prompt-typed-1024x449.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss-prompt-typed-768x337.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss-prompt-typed-360x158.png 360w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1536px) 100vw, 1536px\" \/><figcaption class=\"wp-element-caption\">AI prompt screen with an example description typed and the Generate button ready.<\/figcaption><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Step 2: Review the AI-Generated Form<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">After you submit your prompt, the AI generates the form and displays a real-time preview on the right side of the screen. The left panel shows the AI&#8217;s response explaining the form structure it created.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The preview reflects the actual form as it will appear to your users \u2014 including field labels, placeholders, and layout.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image aligncenter size-full\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1536\" height=\"674\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss-generated.png\" alt=\"Generated contact form preview with chat panel on left and form fields on right\" class=\"wp-image-5708\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss-generated.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss-generated-300x132.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss-generated-1024x449.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss-generated-768x337.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/ss-generated-360x158.png 360w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1536px) 100vw, 1536px\" \/><figcaption class=\"wp-element-caption\">AI-generated form preview with chat panel and form fields.<\/figcaption><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<blockquote class=\"wp-block-quote is-layout-flow wp-block-quote-is-layout-flow\">\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Note:<\/strong> The form preview is for review only. No form is saved to your site until you click <strong>Use This Form<\/strong>.<\/p>\n<\/blockquote>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Refining Your Form<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Chatting with AI to Make Changes<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">If the generated form doesn&#8217;t match your needs exactly, you can request changes through the chat panel on the left. Type your refinement request and the AI will update the preview instantly.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Example refinements:<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><em>&#8220;Add a dropdown for department selection.&#8221;<\/em><\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><em>&#8220;Remove the phone field and make email required.&#8221;<\/em><\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><em>&#8220;Split the form into two sections \u2014 personal info and project details.&#8221;<\/em><\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can make as many refinements as you need before saving.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Starting Over<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">To discard the current form and start fresh with a new prompt, click the <strong>\u2190 New Prompt<\/strong> button in the top-left header. This clears the conversation and returns you to the initial prompt screen.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Saving and Using Your Form<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Once you are satisfied with the generated form, click the <strong>Use This Form<\/strong> button in the header.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">This will:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Save the form to Everest Forms with all fields, labels, and settings intact<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Open the standard form builder so you can make any further manual adjustments<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Allow you to configure email notifications, confirmations, and other settings as usual<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<blockquote class=\"wp-block-quote is-layout-flow wp-block-quote-is-layout-flow\">\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Tip:<\/strong> After clicking <strong>Use This Form<\/strong>, you can continue editing your form in the standard builder \u2014 adding conditional logic, adjusting field settings, or enabling integrations.<\/p>\n<\/blockquote>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Even after saving your AI-generated form, you can access the AI assistant (the sparkle button at the bottom-right of the builder) to continue refining your form.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image aligncenter size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_DyrplYxVwn.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"486\" https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_DyrplYxVwn-1536x729.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/chrome_DyrplYxVwn-360x171.png class=\"wp-block-heading\">Publishing Your Form<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">After saving, you can embed the form anywhere on your site using:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>The <strong>Everest Forms block<\/strong> in the WordPress block editor<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>The shortcode displayed on the form&#8217;s edit page (e.g., <code><div class=\"everest-forms\"><script type='text\/javascript'>\n\/* <![CDATA[ *\/\nvar evf_settings = {\"currency_code\":\"USD\",\"currency_thousands\":\",\",\"currency_decimal\":\".\",\"currency_symbol\":\"$\",\"currency_symbol_pos\":\"left\"}\n\/* ]]> *\/\n<\/script>\n<\/div><\/code>)<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>The <strong>Everest Forms widget<\/strong> in classic widget areas<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">For detailed embedding instructions, see <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/how-to-display-the-forms-you-have-created\/\">How to Display the Forms You Have Created<\/a>.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Frequently Asked Questions<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Can I edit the AI-generated form after saving?<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Yes. Once you click <strong>Use This Form<\/strong>, the form opens in the standard Everest Forms builder where you can add, remove, or modify any fields and settings.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">What if the AI generates the wrong field type?<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can either ask the AI to change it via the chat panel (e.g., <em>&#8220;Change the phone field to a dropdown&#8221;<\/em>) or edit it manually after saving in the standard builder.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><\/p>\n","protected":false},"doc_category":[6]},{"id":5886,"slug":"mollie-via-payment-gateway","link":"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/mollie-via-payment-gateway\/","title":{"rendered":"Mollie"},"content":{"rendered":"\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">This documentation covers the new payment flow using the <strong>Payment Gateway<\/strong> field. If you're using the <strong>Payment Gateway<\/strong> field, continue with this documentation. Otherwise, if you're using the <strong>legacy Payments tab<\/strong>, your existing payment setup will continue to work without any changes. Please refer to our <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/mollie\/\">Legacy Payments documentation<\/a>.<\/pre>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The new <strong>Payment Gateway<\/strong> field lets you enable multiple payment options in one place, including Square, PayPal, Mollie, Authorize.Net, Stripe, and Razorpay. This allows users to choose their preferred payment method and pay the amount configured in your form.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">For <strong>Mollie payments<\/strong>, customers can securely complete transactions using credit\/debit cards, bank transfers, and other supported local payment methods. When a visitor selects Mollie and submits the form, they are redirected to a secure Mollie checkout page to finish the payment and then returned to your site. All transactions are processed safely through Mollie, allowing you to accept both one-time and recurring payments depending on your setup.<br><\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Installation<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>First, you will be required to purchase the <strong>Everest Forms pro<\/strong> plugin.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Once your purchase is verified, the plugin zip file will be made available to you on your WPEverest account page.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Next, download the <strong>Everest form pro<\/strong> plugin zip from your WPEverest account page<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Then, you will be required to Install the Everest pro plugin through your <strong>WordPress admin dashboard<\/strong>. To do this, visit <strong>Plugins \u2192 Add new \u2192 Upload<\/strong> and and then choose the downloaded zip file, and then install and activate it.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Now go to Everest Forms &gt; <strong>Addons <\/strong>&gt; <strong>Payment Gateways<\/strong> &gt; <strong><strong>Mollie<\/strong><\/strong>.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-33.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"432\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-33-1024x432.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5888\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-33-1024x432.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-33-300x126.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-33-768x324.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-33-1536x648.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-33-360x152.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-33.png 1900w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-code\"><code>For a detailed guide, read our documentation on <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/premium-feature-unlock\/\">how to install and activate Everest Forms Pro<\/a>. <\/code><\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Create Mollie API<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You will need the Mollie test or live API keys to integrate it to Everest Forms plugin. For this, follow the steps below.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><a href=\"https:\/\/my.mollie.com\/dashboard\/login\" rel=\"nofollow noopener\" target=\"_blank\">Log in or sign up to Form<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Go to <strong>Everest Forms \u2192 All Forms<\/strong> and open the form you want to accept Mollie payments on (or create a new form).<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>In the form builder, open <strong>Fields \u2192 Payment Fields<\/strong>.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Drag the <strong>Payment Gateway<\/strong> field onto your form.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Click the <strong>Payment Gateway<\/strong> field to open its settings on the left.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>In the Gateways section, make sure the Mollie toggle is <strong>turned on<\/strong>.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-39.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" <strong>single item<\/strong>. You can customize it even further by changing its features. Choose between Field to fit your payment forms better<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-table\"><table><thead><tr><td><strong>Field<\/strong><\/td><td><strong>Use it
