class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">For a simple payment form \u2014 like selling a product or accepting a donation \u2014 here&#8217;s all you need:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Add the <strong>Payment Gateway<\/strong> field and make sure <strong>Mollie <\/strong>is turned on.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Add a Single Item field and set your price.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Add a Total field so visitors can see what they&#8217;ll be charged.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Add an Email field \u2014 Stripe needs an email to send the payment receipt.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>After clicking <strong>Save<\/strong>, copy the generated shortcode and embed it in an existing or newly created page or post.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">When a visitor selects Mollie and submits the form, they enter their card details on the form and are charged immediately. You'll see the payment in your Mollie Dashboard and under Everest Forms \u2192 Entries.<\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Accepting Recurring Payments (Subscriptions)<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Use this if you want to charge visitors automatically on a schedule \u2014 for example, every month or every year.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-20.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"433\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-20-1024x433.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5845\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-20-1024x433.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-20-300x127.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-20-768x325.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-20-1536x650.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-20-360x152.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-20.png 1900w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The Subscription Plan field lets visitors choose between different plans \u2014 for example, Monthly vs Annual.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>For Mollie subscription payments, add the <strong>Payment Gateway<\/strong> field to the form and ensure that Mollie is enabled.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Then, in the form builder, go to <strong>Fields \u2192 Payment Fields<\/strong> and drag <strong>Subscription Plan<\/strong> into your form.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Click the field to open its settings. By default it has 3 plan options \u2014 edit the label and price for each.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>To add more plans click the + button; to remove one click the x button.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>To pre-select a plan, click the circle next to that option.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>To add product images to each plan, turn on Use image choices and upload an image per plan.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Add a trial period<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can let new subscribers try your service for free before their first charge.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Click the Subscription Plan field to open its settings.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Turn on Enable Trial Period.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Set the length \u2014 days, weeks, months, or years.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Add an expiry date<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">If your subscription should stop automatically on a specific date, turn on Enable Expiry Date and pick the date from the calendar.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Send Extra Customer Details to Mollie <\/h3>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-21.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"486\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-21-1024x486.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5848\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-21-1024x486.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-21-300x142.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-21-768x364.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-21-1536x729.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-21-360x171.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-21.png 1920w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">By default, Mollie receives the payment amount and email. You can also send the visitor&#8217;s first name, Last name, address. This makes it easier to identify payments in your Mollie Dashboard.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Click on the Payment Gateway.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Go to <strong>Gateways \u2192 <\/strong>Mollie and click on the accordion.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Use the dropdowns to match each Mollie field (First Name, Last Name, Address) to the matching field on your form.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Click <strong>Save<\/strong>.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Finishing Up<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Click <strong>Save<\/strong> in the top right of the form builder.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Embed the form directly into a page:\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Existing page<\/strong> \u2014 open the page, paste the shortcode where you want the form to appear, and update the page.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>New page<\/strong> \u2014 create a new page, paste the shortcode, and publish it.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Alternatively, copy the shortcode located beside the <strong>Save<\/strong> button, then add a <strong>Shortcode<\/strong> block to any page and paste the shortcode into that block.<br><\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">How to setup webhook for Mollie<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Your Mollie webhook endpoint URL is:<br><code>https:\/\/your-site.com\/?everest_forms_mollie_webhook=webhook<\/code><\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>You do\u00a0not\u00a0need to add this URL manually in the Mollie Dashboard. Everest Forms sends it automatically as\u00a0<code>webhookUrl<\/code>\u00a0when creating each Mollie payment\/subscription.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Make sure your site is publicly reachable (HTTPS). Local sites need a tunnel like ngrok.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>In WordPress, go to\u00a0Everest Forms \u2192 Settings \u2192 Payments\u00a0and expand\u00a0Mollie Settings. Enter your\u00a0Test mode API key\u00a0and\/or\u00a0Live mode API key.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Save your settings.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Note:&nbsp;Mollie does not send webhooks for subscription cancel\/status changes. Everest Forms syncs those via a scheduled cron job (<code>everest_forms_mollie_sync_subscriptions<\/code>) every 15 minutes.<\/p>\n","protected":false},"doc_category":[61]},{"id":5896,"slug":"how-to-use-new-payment-gateway","link":"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/how-to-use-new-payment-gateway\/","title":{"rendered":"How to Use the Payment Gateway Field"},"content":{"rendered":"\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">This documentation covers the new payment flow using the <strong>Payment Gateway<\/strong> field. If you're using the <strong>Payment Gateway<\/strong> field, continue with this documentation. Otherwise, if you're using the <strong>legacy Payments tab<\/strong>, your existing payment setup will continue to work without any changes. Please refer to our <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/authorize-net\/\">Legacy Payments documentation<\/a>.<\/pre>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The Payment Gateway field lets visitors choose how they want to pay \u2014 Stripe, PayPal, Square, Authorize.Net, Mollie, or Razorpay \u2014 all on the same form. Card fields and redirect messages switch automatically based on what the visitor picks.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Looking for a specific classic gateway guide instead? See <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/paypal-standard\/\">PayPal Standard<\/a>, <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/stripe\/\">Stripe<\/a>, <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/square\/\">Square<\/a>, <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/authorize-net\/\">Authorize.Net<\/a>, <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/mollie\/\">Mollie<\/a>, or <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/razorpay\/\">Razorpay<\/a>.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-video\"><video controls src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/6a445c74-ac59-494a-84b1-be0c7dcd34fe-1.mp4\"><\/video><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">What&#8217;s New?<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">With <strong>Everest Forms Pro 1.9.17+<\/strong>, the Payment Gateway field is available under <strong>Fields \u2192 Payment Fields<\/strong> in the form builder.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-34.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"431\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-34-1024x431.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5897\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-34-1024x431.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-34-300x126.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-34-768x323.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-34-1536x647.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-34-360x152.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-34.png 1903w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>One field per form.<\/strong> Only one Payment Gateway field can be added to a form. Once added, the legacy Credit Card, Square Payment, and Authorize.Net fields are hidden from the field list to prevent duplicate payment UIs.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><br><strong>Supported gateways:<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-35.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"433\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-35-1024x433.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5900\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-35-1024x433.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-35-300x127.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-35-768x325.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-35-1536x650.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-35-360x152.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-35.png 1894w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-table\"><table class=\"has-fixed-layout\"><thead><tr><th>Gateway<\/th><th>Add-on<\/th><th>Checkout type<\/th><\/tr><\/thead><tbody><tr><td>Stripe<\/td><td>Everest Forms Stripe<\/td><td>On-page card<\/td><\/tr><tr><td>PayPal Standard<\/td><td>Everest Forms PayPal Standard<\/td><td>Redirect to PayPal<\/td><\/tr><tr><td>Square<\/td><td>Everest Forms Square (Pro)<\/td><td>On-page card<\/td><\/tr><tr><td>Authorize.Net<\/td><td>Everest Forms Authorize.Net<\/td><td>On-page card<\/td><\/tr><tr><td>Mollie<\/td><td>Everest Forms Mollie (Pro)<\/td><td>Redirect to Mollie<\/td><\/tr><tr><td>Razorpay<\/td><td>Everest Forms Razorpay<\/td><td>Redirect to Razorpay<\/td><\/tr><\/tbody><\/table><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">A gateway appears on the form only when its add-on is active, global credentials are saved, and the gateway is enabled in the field&#8217;s Gateways list.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Installation<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>First, you will be required to purchase the&nbsp;<strong>Everest Forms pro<\/strong>&nbsp;plugin.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Once your purchase is verified, the plugin zip file will be made available to you on your WPEverest account page.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Next, download the&nbsp;<strong>Everest form pro<\/strong>&nbsp;plugin zip as well as the&nbsp;<strong>addons<\/strong>&nbsp;from your WPEverest account page.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Then, you will be required to Install the Everest pro plugin and the addons through your&nbsp;<strong>WordPress admin dashboard<\/strong>. To do this, visit&nbsp;<strong>Plugins \u2192 Add new \u2192 Upload<\/strong>&nbsp;and then choose the downloaded zip files. Once the plugins have been&nbsp;<strong>installed<\/strong>,&nbsp;<strong>activate<\/strong>&nbsp;them and your addons should be enabled on your website.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">For step-by-step installation help see the install guide<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Step 1 \u2014 Connect Your Payment Accounts<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The Payment Gateway field does not replace global payment settings. Each add-on still needs credentials saved once site-wide.<br>Go to <strong>Everest Forms \u2192 Settings \u2192 Payments<\/strong> <strong>\u2192<\/strong> <strong>Payment Method<\/strong> and fill in the details for each gateway:<br><\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-table\"><table class=\"has-fixed-layout\"><thead><tr><th>Gateway<\/th><th>What to enter<\/th><\/tr><\/thead><tbody><tr><td>Stripe<\/td><td>Publishable key and Secret key (Live\/Test)<\/td><\/tr><tr><td>PayPal Standard<\/td><td>PayPal email, Mode (Sandbox\/Production), Payment type<\/td><\/tr><tr><td>Square<\/td><td>Application ID, Access token, Location ID<\/td><\/tr><tr><td>Authorize.Net<\/td><td>API Login ID and Transaction Key<\/td><\/tr><tr><td>Mollie<\/td><td>API key<\/td><\/tr><tr><td>Razorpay<\/td><td>Key ID and Secret<\/td><\/tr><\/tbody><\/table><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Also set your <strong>Currency<\/strong> under the General payments section.<br><\/p>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Step 2 \u2014 Add the Payment Gateway Field to Your Form<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Go to <strong>Everest Forms \u2192 All Forms<\/strong> and open your form or create a new one.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>In the form builder, open <strong>Fields \u2192 Payment Fields<\/strong>.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Drag <strong>Payment Gateway<\/strong> onto your form.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Add your payment fields \u2014 Single Item, Multiple Choice, Total, etc.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Click <strong>Save<\/strong>.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">The field is required by default \u2014 visitors must pick a payment method before submitting.<\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Step 3 \u2014 Choose Which Gateways to Show<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Click the <strong>Payment Gateway<\/strong> field to open its settings.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>In <strong>Field Options<\/strong>, open <strong>Gateways<\/strong>.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Toggle each gateway on or off. Drag to reorder them.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Click the arrow (\u203a) next to any gateway to open its per-gateway settings.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\"><strong>Good to know:<\/strong><br>1. Gateways without saved credentials appear as disabled until connected globally.<br>2. PayPal is off by default if no global PayPal email is saved.<br>3. You need at least one gateway turned on \u2014 the form will remind you if all are off.<br>4. You do not need to enable PayPal or Stripe on the legacy Payments tab when using this field<\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Per-Gateway Options<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Expand any gateway&#8217;s accordion (\u203a) to access settings specific to that provider:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-table\"><table class=\"has-fixed-layout\"><thead><tr><th>Gateway<\/th><th>Options<\/th><\/tr><\/thead><tbody><tr><td>PayPal<\/td><td>Per-form email, Sandbox\/Production mode, Cancel URL<\/td><\/tr><tr><td>Stripe<\/td><td>Recurring toggle, iDEAL, map customer fields<\/td><\/tr><tr><td>Authorize.Net<\/td><td>Map Customer Email (required for subscriptions)<\/td><\/tr><tr><td>Square<\/td><td>Subscription options<\/td><\/tr><tr><td>Mollie<\/td><td>Subscription description and recurring defaults<\/td><\/tr><\/tbody><\/table><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Payment Fields<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Add one or more of these to your form:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-table\"><table class=\"has-fixed-layout\"><thead><tr><th>Field<\/th><th>Use it when\u2026<\/th><\/tr><\/thead><tbody><tr><td><strong>Single Item<\/strong><\/td><td>Selling one thing at a fixed price<\/td><\/tr><tr><td><strong>Single Item (User Defined)<\/strong><\/td><td>Visitor enters their own amount \u2014 great for donations<\/td><\/tr><tr><td><strong>Single Item (Hidden)<\/strong><\/td><td>Price is charged silently without showing on the form<\/td><\/tr><tr><td><strong>Multiple Choice<\/strong><\/td><td>Visitor picks one option from a list, each with its own price<\/td><\/tr><tr><td><strong>Checkbox<\/strong><\/td><td>Visitor selects more than one item at the same time<\/td><\/tr><tr><td><strong>Quantity<\/strong><\/td><td>Visitor enters how many of an item they want<\/td><\/tr><tr><td><strong>Total<\/strong><\/td><td>Shows the running total of everything selected<\/td><\/tr><\/tbody><\/table><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Accepting a One-Time Payment<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-36.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"434\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-36-1024x434.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5901\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-36-1024x434.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-36-300x127.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-36-768x325.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-36-1536x650.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-36-360x152.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-36.png 1892w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Add the Payment Gateway field and enable Stripe, PayPal, or any combination.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Add a <strong>Single Item<\/strong> field and set your price.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Add a <strong>Total<\/strong> field so visitors see what they&#8217;ll be charged.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Add contact fields (Name, Email, etc.).<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Click <strong>Save<\/strong> and embed the form.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">What visitors see:<br>1. Stripe \/ Square \/ Authorize.Net \u2014 a card entry form appears on the page when they select that gateway.<br>2. PayPal \/ Mollie \u2014 a redirect message appears and they complete payment on the provider's site.<br>3. Razorpay \u2014 a secure payment popup\/modal opens on top of the checkout page, where customers complete payment without leaving the site.<br><\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Accepting Recurring Payments (Subscriptions)<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-37.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"432\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-37-1024x432.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5902\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-37-1024x432.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-37-300x127.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-37-768x324.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-37-1536x648.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-37-360x152.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-37.png 1894w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Add the Payment Gateway field and enable gateways that support subscriptions.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Add a <strong>Subscription Plan<\/strong> field and configure plans \u2014 price, billing period, trial, and expiry.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Open each gateway&#8217;s accordion (\u203a) and map any required fields (e.g. Authorize.Net requires Customer Email to be mapped).<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Click <strong>Save<\/strong>.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">When both Payment Gateway and Subscription Plan are on the form, Everest Forms automatically treats checkout as subscription mode. You do not need to turn on recurring payments on the legacy Payments tab.<\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">What Visitors See<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-38.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"549\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-38-1024x549.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5903\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-38-1024x549.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-38-300x161.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-38-768x412.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-38-1536x824.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-38-360x193.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-38.png 1588w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>A card grid of payment method logos on the form.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>If only one gateway is enabled it is selected automatically.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Stripe, Square, Authorize.Net \u2014 card input appears inline below the gateway cards.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>PayPal, Mollie, Razorpay \u2014 a secure redirect message is shown after submitting.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><\/p>\n","protected":false},"doc_category":[61]},{"id":5874,"slug":"razorpay-via-payment-gateway","link":"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/razorpay-via-payment-gateway\/","title":{"rendered":"RazorPay"},"content":{"rendered":"\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">This documentation covers the new payment flow using the <strong>Payment Gateway<\/strong> field. If you're using the <strong>Payment Gateway<\/strong> field, continue with this documentation. Otherwise, if you're using the <strong>legacy Payments tab<\/strong>, your existing payment setup will continue to work without any changes. Please refer to our <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/razorpay\/\">Legacy Payments documentation<\/a>.<\/pre>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The new <strong>Payment Gateway<\/strong> field lets you enable multiple payment options in one place, including Stripe, PayPal, Mollie, Authorize.Net, Square, and Razorpay. This allows users to choose their preferred payment method and pay the amount configured in your form.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">For <strong>Razorpay payments<\/strong>, the Everest Forms add-on allows you to seamlessly integrate the Indian payment gateway into your forms. Start taking payments via credit\/debit card, net banking, or popular Indian mobile wallets today. Integrate the Everest Forms payment module with Razorpay using this add-on\u2014quickly, securely, and with complete confidence in transaction safety.<br><\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Installation<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Installation<\/strong> of the Everest form RazorPay addon requires the following steps:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>First, you will be required to purchase the <strong>Everest Forms pro<\/strong> plugin.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Once your purchase is verified, the plugin zip file will be made available to you on your WPEverest account page.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Next, download the <strong>Everest form pro<\/strong> plugin zip as well as the <strong><strong>RazorPay<\/strong> addon<\/strong> from your WPEverest account page<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Then, you will be required to Install the Everest pro plugin and the <strong>RazorPay <\/strong>addon through your <strong>WordPress admin dashboard<\/strong>. To do this, visit <strong>Plugins \u2192 Add new \u2192 Upload<\/strong> and then choose the downloaded zip files. Once the plugins have been <strong>installed<\/strong>, <strong>activate<\/strong> them and your addon should be enabled on your website.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-27.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"438\" Configuration<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">For API Keys:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Go To <strong><a href=\"https:\/\/dashboard.razorpay.com\/#\/access\/signin\" account, <strong>log in, <\/strong>else,<strong> signup.<\/strong><\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"958\" height=\"650\" <\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"470\" Key<\/strong>.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>The&nbsp;<strong>Client Id&nbsp;<\/strong>and<strong>&nbsp;Secret Key<\/strong>&nbsp;will appear. Therefore, it is recommended to download the keys.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"467\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/05\/ApI-Generation-1024x467.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1472\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/05\/ApI-Generation-1024x467.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/05\/ApI-Generation-300x137.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/05\/ApI-Generation-768x351.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/05\/ApI-Generation-360x164.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/05\/ApI-Generation.png 1365w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Integrating RazorPay API with Everest Forms<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">To make sure the <strong>RazorPay<\/strong> addon and the Payments options work perfectly on your website, we suggest you tune its settings to fit your forms better. To open the Payments settings, visit <strong>Everest Forms <strong>\u2192<\/strong> Settings <\/strong>through your admin sidebar. Next, click on the <strong>Payments<\/strong> <strong><strong>\u2192<\/strong><\/strong> <strong>Payment Method<\/strong> section inside payments settings.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-28.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"438\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-28-1024x438.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5877\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-28-1024x438.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-28-300x128.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-28-768x328.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-28-1536x657.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-28-360x154.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-28.png 1904w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Once you\u2019ve installed and activated the add-on, you\u2019ll need to enable Razorpay integration within Everest Forms. To complete the setup, you must use your Razorpay account credentials. Follow the steps above to obtain your API keys.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">In the Razorpay settings, enter your Publishable Key and Secret Key in the designated fields. If you\u2019d like to test the payment flow before going live, you can use the test keys provided by Razorpay.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">To enable testing, simply turn on the <strong>Enable Test Mode<\/strong> option. In test mode, you can use your Razorpay test credentials to simulate transactions. You can also refer to the official documentation to learn more about test keys and access sample test credit card details for validation.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Hit the <strong>Save Changes <\/strong>button when you complete the settings<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Add RazorPay to Your Form<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Go to <strong>Everest Forms \u2192 All Forms<\/strong> and open the form you want to accept RazorPay payments on (or create a new form).<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>In the form builder, open <strong>Fields \u2192 Payment Fields<\/strong>.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Drag the <strong>Payment Gateway<\/strong> field onto your form.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Click the <strong>Payment Gateway<\/strong> field to open its settings on the left.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>In the Gateways section, make sure the <strong>RazorPay <\/strong>toggle is <strong>turned on<\/strong>.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-29.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"445\" https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-29-768x333.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-29-1536x667.png 1536w, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">When a visitor selects Razorpay and submits the form, they are redirected to Razorpay to complete the payment. After a successful transaction, they are returned to your site and the payment is logged under Everest Forms \u2192 Entries.<\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Add what you&#8217;re charging for<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Set the price on a <strong>single item<\/strong>. You can customize it even further by changing its features. Choose between Field to fit your payment forms better<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-table\"><table><thead><tr><td><strong>Field<\/strong><\/td><td><strong>Use it when\u2026<\/strong><\/td><\/tr><\/thead><tbody><tr><td><strong>Single Item<\/strong><\/td><td>You&#8217;re selling one product or service at a fixed price<\/td><\/tr><tr><td><strong>Single Item (User Defined)<\/strong><\/td><td>You want visitors to enter their own amount \u2014 great for donations<\/td><\/tr><tr><td><strong>Multiple Choice<\/strong><\/td><td>Visitors pick one option from a list (e.g. Small, Medium, Large)<\/td><\/tr><tr><td><strong>Checkbox<\/strong><\/td><td>Visitors can select more than one item at the same time<\/td><\/tr><tr><td><strong>Subscription Plan<\/strong><\/td><td>Visitors choose a recurring billing plan (monthly, yearly, etc.)<\/td><\/tr><tr><td><strong>Total<\/strong><\/td><td>Shows the running total of everything selected \u2014 always add this for multi-item forms<\/td><\/tr><\/tbody><\/table><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-32-768x364.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-32-1536x729.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-32-360x171.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-32.png 1920w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Accepting a One-Time Payment<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">For a simple payment form \u2014 like selling a product or accepting a donation \u2014 here&#8217;s all you need:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Add the <strong>Payment Gateway<\/strong> field and make sure <strong>RazorPay<\/strong> is turned on.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Add a Single Item field and set your price.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Add a Total field so visitors can see what they&#8217;ll be charged.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Add an Email field \u2014 RazorPay needs an email to send the payment receipt.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>After clicking <strong>Save<\/strong>, copy the generated shortcode and embed it in an existing or newly created page or post.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">When a visitor selects RazorPay and submits the form, they enter their card details on the form and are charged immediately. You'll see the payment in your RazorPay Dashboard and under Everest Forms \u2192 Entries.<\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Accepting Recurring Payments (Subscriptions)<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Use this if you want to charge visitors automatically on a schedule \u2014 for example, every month or every year.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-31.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"441\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-31-1024x441.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5883\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-31-1024x441.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-31-300x129.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-31-768x331.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-31-1536x662.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-31-360x155.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-31.png 1900w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The Subscription Plan field lets visitors choose between different plans \u2014 for example, Monthly vs Annual.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>For RazorPay subscription payments, add the <strong>Payment Gateway<\/strong> field to the form and ensure that <strong>RazorPay<\/strong> is enabled.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Then, in the form builder, go to <strong>Fields \u2192 Payment Fields<\/strong> and drag <strong>Subscription Plan<\/strong> into your form.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Click the field to open its settings. By default it has 3 plan options \u2014 edit the label and price for each.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>To add more plans click the + button; to remove one click the x button.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>To pre-select a plan, click the circle next to that option.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>To add product images to each plan, turn on Use image choices and upload an image per plan.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Add a trial period<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can let new subscribers try your service for free before their first charge.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Click the Subscription Plan field to open its settings.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Turn on Enable Trial Period.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Set the length \u2014 days, weeks, months, or years.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Add an expiry date<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">If your subscription should stop automatically on a specific date, turn on Enable Expiry Date and pick the date from the calendar.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Finishing Up<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Click <strong>Save<\/strong> in the top right of the form builder.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Embed the form directly into a page:\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Existing page<\/strong> \u2014 open the page, paste the shortcode where you want the form to appear, and update the page.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>New page<\/strong> \u2014 create a new page, paste the shortcode, and publish it.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Alternatively, copy the shortcode located beside the <strong>Save<\/strong> button, then add a <strong>Shortcode<\/strong> block to any page and paste the shortcode into that block.<br><\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">How to setup webhook for Razorpay<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Copy your Razorpay webhook endpoint URL:<br><code>https:\/\/your-site.com\/?evf-razorpay-webhook=1<\/code><\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Log in to your Razorpay Dashboard and go to\u00a0Account &amp; Settings \u2192 Webhooks\u00a0(or\u00a0Developers \u2192 Webhooks).<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Click\u00a0Add New Webhook\u00a0and paste the URL above into the\u00a0Webhook URL\u00a0field.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Under\u00a0Active Events, select these events, then click\u00a0Create Webhook:\n<ul If you're using the <strong>Payment Gateway<\/strong> field, continue with this documentation. Otherwise, if you're using the <strong>legacy Payments tab<\/strong>, your existing payment setup will continue to work without any changes. Please refer to our <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/authorize-net\/\">Legacy Payments documentation<\/a>.<\/pre>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The new <strong>Payment Gateway<\/strong> field lets you enable multiple payment options in one place, including Authorize, PayPal, Mollie, Stripe, Square, and Razorpay. This allows users to choose their preferred payment method and pay the amount configured in your form.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">If you want to accept payments securely on your website, the <strong>Authorize.Net add-on<\/strong> for Everest Forms is a reliable solution. It allows your users to pay directly on your forms using credit cards without being redirected away from your site. It supports both one-time payments and recurring subscriptions, making it a flexible option for managing online transactions.<br><\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Installation<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Installation<\/strong> of the Everest form Authorize.net addon requires the following steps:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>First, you will be required to purchase the <strong>Everest Forms pro<\/strong> plugin.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Once your purchase is verified, the plugin zip file will be made available to you on your WPEverest account page.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Next, download the <strong>Everest form pro<\/strong> plugin zip as well as the <strong><strong>Authorize.net<\/strong><\/strong> <strong>addon<\/strong> from your WPEverest account page<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Then, you will be required to Install the Everest pro plugin and the Authorize.net addon through your <strong>WordPress admin dashboard<\/strong>. To do this, visit <strong>Plugins \u2192 Add new \u2192 Upload<\/strong> and then choose the downloaded zip files. Once the plugins have been <strong>installed<\/strong>, <strong>activate<\/strong> them and your addon should be enabled on your website.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-22.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"440\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-22-1024x440.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5860\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-22-1024x440.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-22-300x129.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-22-768x330.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-22-1536x660.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-22-360x155.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-22.png 1898w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-code\"><code>For a detailed guide, read our documentation on <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/premium-feature-unlock\/\">how to install and activate Everest Forms Pro<\/a>. <\/code><\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Getting API Keys<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can use either Live or Test APIs with the Authorize.net payment system. To generate the API, follow the steps below.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Sign In to the <a href=\"https:\/\/account.authorize.net\/\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noreferrer noopener nofollow\">Authorize.net account<\/a>.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>If you don&#8217;t have an account, <a href=\"https:\/\/www.authorize.net\/sign-up\/pricing.html\" target=\"_blank\" data-type=\"URL\" data-id=\"https:\/\/www.authorize.net\/sign-up\/pricing.html\" rel=\"noreferrer noopener nofollow\">create one<\/a>.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Once you are logged in, you will see a popup from which you can toggle between live or test mode.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/06\/image-1-3.png\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/06\/image-1-3.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2736\"\/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>You can remove the popup from the available buttons and then go to<strong> Account &gt; Settings<\/strong>.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/06\/image-2-2.png\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/06\/image-2-2.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2737\"\/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Inside Settings, click on <strong>API Credentials &amp; Keys<\/strong>. Here, you will find the necessary keys.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/06\/image-3-1.png\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/06\/image-3-1.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2738\"\/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>You will need an API Login ID and a Transaction key. When you create an account, you will be given with both of these keys in the beginning.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/06\/image-4-2.png\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/06\/image-4-2.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2739\"\/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">In this tutorial, we assume that you have to create a new transaction key. For this, you can click on the &#8216;<strong>New Transaction Key<\/strong>&#8216; option and click on <strong>Submit.<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/06\/image-5.png\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/06\/image-5.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2740\"\/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">In this tutorial, we assume that you have to create a new transaction key. For this, you can click on the &#8216;<strong>New Transaction Key<\/strong>&#8216; option and click on <strong>Submit.<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/06\/image-5.png\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/06\/image-5.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2740\"\/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">After clicking on the submit button, the app will require you to put the verification key sent to your email. You can request this key by clicking on the &#8216;Request Pin&#8217; button on the pop-up.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/06\/image-6.png\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/06\/image-6.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2741\"\/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Once you\u2019ve verified your identity, Authorize.Net will display your Transaction Key. Click the Copy to Clipboard button.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Now, after copying both the API login key and the Transaction Key, you can now connect the Everest Forms with the Authorize.net payment portal.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Setup and Configuration<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">To make sure the Authorize.net addon and the Payments options work perfectly on your website, we suggest you tune its settings to fit your forms better. To open the Payments settings, visit <strong>Everest Forms <strong>\u2192<\/strong> Settings <\/strong>through your admin sidebar. Next, click on the <strong>Payments<\/strong> <strong><strong>\u2192<\/strong><\/strong> <strong>Payment Method<\/strong> section inside payments settings.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image \u2192 All Forms<\/strong> and open the form you want to accept Authorize.net payments on (or create a new form).<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>In the form builder, open <strong>Fields \u2192 Payment Fields<\/strong>.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Drag the <strong>Payment Gateway<\/strong> field onto your form.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Click the <strong>Payment Gateway<\/strong> field to open its settings on the left.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>In the Gateways section, make sure the <strong>Authorize.net<\/strong> toggle is <strong>turned on<\/strong>.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image extra setup needed.<\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Add what you&#8217;re charging for<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Set the price on a <strong>single item<\/strong>. You can customize it even further by changing its features. Choose between Field to fit your payment forms better<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-table\"><table><thead><tr><td><strong>Field<\/strong><\/td><td><strong>Use it (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Accepting a One-Time Payment<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">For a simple payment form \u2014 like selling a product or accepting a donation \u2014 here&#8217;s all you need:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Add the <strong>Payment Gateway<\/strong> field and make sure <strong>Authorize.net<\/strong> is turned on.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Add a Single Item field and set your price.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Add a Total field so visitors can see what they&#8217;ll be charged.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Add an Email field \u2014 Authorize.net needs an email to send the payment receipt.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>After clicking <strong>Save<\/strong>, copy the generated shortcode and embed it in an existing or newly created page or post.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">When a visitor selects Authorize.net and submits the form, they enter their card details on the form and are charged immediately. You'll see the payment in your Authorize.net Dashboard and under Everest Forms \u2192 Entries.<\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Accepting Recurring Payments (Subscriptions)<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Use this if you want to charge visitors automatically on a schedule \u2014 for example, every month or every year.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-40.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"437\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-40-1024x437.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5922\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-40-1024x437.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-40-300x128.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-40-768x328.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-40-1536x656.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-40-360x154.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-40.png 1894w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The Subscription Plan field lets visitors choose between different plans \u2014 for example, Monthly vs Annual.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>For Authorize.net subscription payments, add the <strong>Payment Gateway<\/strong> field to the form and ensure that <strong>Authorize.net<\/strong> is enabled.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Use the dropdowns to map <strong>Authorize.Net<\/strong> fields to your form. <strong>Email is required<\/strong>, while other fields like Payment Description, First Name, Last Name, Company Name, and Billing Address are optional.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Then, in the form builder, go to <strong>Fields \u2192 Payment Fields<\/strong> and drag <strong>Subscription Plan<\/strong> into your form.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Click the field to open its settings. By default it has 3 plan options \u2014 edit the label and price for each.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>To add more plans click the + button; to remove one click the x button.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>To pre-select a plan, click the circle next to that option.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>To add product images to each plan, turn on Use image choices and upload an image per plan.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Add a trial period<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can let new subscribers try your service for free before their first charge.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Click the Subscription Plan field to open its settings.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Turn on Enable Trial Period.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Set the length \u2014 days, weeks, months, or years.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Add an expiry date<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">If your subscription should stop automatically on a specific date, turn on Enable Expiry Date and pick the date from the calendar.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Send Extra Customer Details to Authorize.net<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-26.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"426\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-26-1024x426.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5870\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-26-1024x426.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-26-300x125.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-26-768x320.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-26-1536x639.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-26-360x150.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-26.png 1896w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">By default, Authorize.net receives the payment amount and email. You can also send the visitor&#8217;s name, phone number, address, and a transaction description. This makes it easier to identify payments in your Authorize.net Dashboard.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Click on the Payment Gateway<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Go to <strong>Gateways \u2192 Authorize.net<\/strong> and click on the accordion.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Use the dropdowns to match each Authorize.net field (Payment Description, Payment Recepit Mail, Email Subscriptions , First Name, Last Name, Company Name, Billing Address) to the matching field on your form.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Click Save.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Finishing Up<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Click <strong>Save<\/strong> in the top right of the form builder.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Embed the form directly into a page:\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Existing page<\/strong> \u2014 open the page, paste the shortcode where you want the form to appear, and update the page.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>New page<\/strong> \u2014 create a new page, paste the shortcode, and publish it.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Alternatively, copy the shortcode located beside the <strong>Save<\/strong> button, then add a <strong>Shortcode<\/strong> block to any page and paste the shortcode into that block.<br><\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">How to setup webhook for AUthorize.Net<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Copy your Authorize.Net webhook endpoint URL:<code>https:\/\/your-site.com\/?evf-authorize-net-webhook=1<\/code><\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Log in to the&nbsp;<strong>Authorize.Net Merchant Interface<\/strong>&nbsp;and go to&nbsp;<strong>Account \u2192 Business Settings \u2192 Webhooks<\/strong>.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Click&nbsp;<strong>Add Endpoint<\/strong>, give it a name, and paste the URL above into the&nbsp;<strong>Endpoint URL<\/strong>&nbsp;field.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Set&nbsp;<strong>Status<\/strong>&nbsp;to&nbsp;<strong>Active<\/strong>, select the events below, and save.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Copy the&nbsp;<strong>Signature Key<\/strong>&nbsp;from&nbsp;<strong>Account \u2192 Settings \u2192 API Credentials &amp; Keys<\/strong>&nbsp;(or from the webhook screen).<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>In WordPress, go to&nbsp;<strong>Everest Forms \u2192 Settings \u2192 Payments \u2192 Payment Method<\/strong>&nbsp;and expand&nbsp;<strong>Authorize.Net Settings<\/strong>. Paste the key into the&nbsp;<strong>Live Webhook Signature Key<\/strong>&nbsp;field (or&nbsp;<strong>Test Webhook Signature Key<\/strong>&nbsp;for the sandbox).<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Save your settings.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">Note: Webhooks are rejected until the Signature Key is saved in Everest Forms, so this step is required \u2014 not optional. If you're using the <strong>Payment Gateway<\/strong> field, continue with this documentation. Otherwise, if you're using the <strong>legacy Payments tab<\/strong>, your existing payment setup will continue to work without any changes. Please refer to our <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/square\/\">Legacy Payments documentation<\/a>.<\/pre>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The new <strong>Payment Gateway<\/strong> field lets you enable multiple payment options in one place, including Square, PayPal, Mollie, Authorize.Net, Stripe, and Razorpay. This allows users to choose their preferred payment method and pay the amount configured in your form.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">For Square payments, the <strong>Square<\/strong> allows you to accept credit and debit card payments directly on your forms. Customers can enter their payment details securely without leaving your site, and transactions are processed through Square. You can collect one-time payments or set up one-time and recurring payments depending on your setup.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Installation<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>First, you will be required to purchase the <strong>Everest Forms pro<\/strong> plugin.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Once your purchase is verified, the plugin zip file will be made available to you on your WPEverest account page.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Next, download the <strong>Everest form pro<\/strong> plugin zip from your WPEverest account page<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Then, you will be required to Install the Everest pro plugin through your <strong>WordPress admin dashboard<\/strong>. To do this, visit <strong>Plugins \u2192 Add new \u2192 Upload<\/strong> and and then choose the downloaded zip file, and then install and activate it.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Now go to Everest Forms &gt; <strong>Addons <\/strong>&gt; <strong>Payment Gateways<\/strong> &gt; <strong>Square<\/strong>.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-16.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"436\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-16-1024x436.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5831\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-16-1024x436.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-16-300x128.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-16-768x327.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-16-1536x654.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-16-360x153.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-16.png 1896w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-code\"><code>For a detailed guide, read our documentation on <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/premium-feature-unlock\/\">how to install and activate Everest Forms Pro<\/a>. <\/code><\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Configuration Sqaure<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">To integrate Square with the Everest Forms, you will need to have an Application ID, Access Token, and Location ID from your Square account.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Generating an Application ID, Access Token, and Location ID<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Go to <a href=\"https:\/\/developer.squareup.com\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noreferrer noopener nofollow\">https:\/\/developer.squareup.com<\/a>.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Log in or sign up to a developer account. <\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Open an application. class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">For a simple payment form \u2014 like selling a product or accepting a donation \u2014 here&#8217;s all you need:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Add the <strong>Payment Gateway<\/strong> field and make sure <strong>Square <\/strong>is turned on.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Add a Single Item field and set your price.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Add a Total field so visitors can see what they&#8217;ll be charged.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Add an Email field \u2014 Stripe needs an email to send the payment receipt.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>After clicking <strong>Save<\/strong>, copy the generated shortcode and embed it in an existing or newly created page or post.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">When a visitor selects Square and submits the form, they enter their card details on the form and are charged immediately. You'll see the payment in your Square Dashboard and under Everest Forms \u2192 Entries.<\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Accepting Recurring Payments (Subscriptions)<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Use this if you want to charge visitors automatically on a schedule \u2014 for example, every month or every year.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-20.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"433\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-20-1024x433.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5845\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-20-1024x433.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-20-300x127.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-20-768x325.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-20-1536x650.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-20-360x152.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-20.png 1900w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The Subscription Plan field lets visitors choose between different plans \u2014 for example, Monthly vs Annual.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>For Square subscription payments, add the <strong>Payment Gateway<\/strong> field to the form and ensure that Square is enabled.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Then, in the form builder, go to <strong>Fields \u2192 Payment Fields<\/strong> and drag <strong>Subscription Plan<\/strong> into your form.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Click the field to open its settings. By default it has 3 plan options \u2014 edit the label and price for each.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>To add more plans click the + button; to remove one click the x button.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>To pre-select a plan, click the circle next to that option.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>To add product images to each plan, turn on Use image choices and upload an image per plan.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Add a trial period<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can let new subscribers try your service for free before their first charge.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Click the Subscription Plan field to open its settings.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Turn on Enable Trial Period.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Set the length \u2014 days, weeks, months, or years.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Add an expiry date<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">If your subscription should stop automatically on a specific date, turn on Enable Expiry Date and pick the date from the calendar.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Send Extra Customer Details to Square<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-21.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" Square<\/strong> and click on the accordion.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Use the dropdowns to match each Square field (First Name, Last Name, Address) to the matching field on your form.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Click builder.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Embed the form directly into a page:\n<ul style=\"margin-top:0;margin-bottom:0\" class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Existing page<\/strong> \u2014 open the page, paste the it.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Alternatively, copy the shortcode located beside the <strong>Save<\/strong> button, then add a <strong>Shortcode<\/strong> block to any page and paste the shortcode into that block.<br><\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">How to setup webhook for Square<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Copy your Square webhook endpoint URL:<br><code>https:\/\/your-site.com\/?evf-square-webhook=1<\/code><\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Log in to the\u00a0<a href=\"https:\/\/developer.squareup.com\/apps\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noreferrer noopener nofollow\">Square Developer Dashboard<\/a>, open your application, and go to\u00a0Webhooks.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Click\u00a0Add Endpoint\u00a0and paste the URL above into the\u00a0Notification URL\u00a0field.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Subscribe to these event types, then save:\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><code>payment.created<\/code><\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><code>payment.updated<\/code><\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><code>subscription.created<\/code><\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><code>subscription.updated<\/code><\/li>\n<\/ul>\n<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Square generates a\u00a0Signature Key. Copy it.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>In WordPress, go to\u00a0Everest Forms \u2192 Settings \u2192 Payments\u00a0and expand\u00a0Square Settings. Paste the key into\u00a0Live Webhook Signature Key\u00a0(or\u00a0Test Webhook Signature Key\u00a0if you\u2019re in test mode).<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Save your settings.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">Note: Square uses separate credentials and signature keys for Sandbox and Production. Match the signature key to the mode set by Enable Test Mode in Square Settings.<\/pre>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><\/p>\n","protected":false},"doc_category":[61]},{"id":5765,"slug":"paypal-via-payment-gateway","link":"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/paypal-via-payment-gateway\/","title":{"rendered":"PayPal Standard"},"content":{"rendered":"\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">This documentation covers the new payment flow using the <strong>Payment Gateway<\/strong> field. If you're using the <strong>Payment Gateway<\/strong> field, continue with this documentation. Otherwise, if you're using the <strong>legacy Payments tab<\/strong>, your existing payment setup will continue to work without any changes. Please refer to our <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/paypal-standard\/\">Legacy Payments documentation<\/a>.<\/pre>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The new <strong>Payment Gateway<\/strong> field lets you enable multiple payment options in one place, including Paypal, Stripe, Mollie, Authorize.Net, Square, and Razorpay. This allows users to choose their preferred payment method and pay the amount configured in your form.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">For PayPal payments, the <strong>PayPal Standard<\/strong> addon lets visitors pay using their PayPal account directly from your form. After submission, they are redirected to PayPal to complete the payment securely and then returned to your site. You can collect one-time payments or set up recurring subscriptions.<br><\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Installation<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Installation<\/strong> of the Everest form PayPal addon requires the following steps:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>First, you will be required to purchase the <strong>Everest Forms pro<\/strong> plugin.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Once your purchase is verified, the plugin zip file will be made available to you on your WPEverest account page.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Next, download the <strong>Everest form pro<\/strong> plugin zip as well as the <strong>PayPal Standard addon<\/strong> from your WPEverest account page<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Then, you will be required to Install the Everest form pro plugin and the PayPal addon through your <strong>WordPress admin dashboard<\/strong>. To do this, visit <strong>Plugins \u2192 Add new \u2192 Upload<\/strong> and then choose the downloaded zip files. Once the plugins have been <strong>installed<\/strong>, <strong>activate<\/strong> them and your addon should be enabled on your website.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-7.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"434\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-7-1024x434.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5767\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-7-1024x434.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-7-300x127.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-7-768x325.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-7-1536x651.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-7-360x153.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-7.png 1900w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-code\"><code>For a detailed guide, read our documentation on <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/premium-feature-unlock\/\">how to install and activate Everest Forms Pro<\/a>. <\/code><\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">PayPal Global Settings<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The <strong>Global PayPal Settings<\/strong> allow you to configure PayPal parameters once and apply them to all forms, eliminating the need to set up PayPal credentials for each form. After you enable the Paypal addon, you can click on the settings icon to access the settings:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-10.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"426\" account: <\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>PayPal Email Address: <\/strong>Enter your primary PayPal business account email. This email will receive all payment notifications across your site and must be registered with PayPal.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Mode: <\/strong>Choose between <strong>Production and Sandbox mode<\/strong>.<br>Production mode is used for live transactions involving real money, while sandbox mode is used for testing and development purposes.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Payment Type:<\/strong> Choose between Product &amp; Service and Donation Payments. <br>Product &amp; Service is suitable for selling products, services, or digital downloads and provides a standard <strong>PayPal<\/strong> checkout experience. On the other hand, <strong>Donation Payment<\/strong> is optimized for charitable organizations and fundraising.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Cancel URL: <\/strong>Define the location to which users are redirected if they cancel the PayPal payment. So add the URL to your homepage, customer support, or anything that you feel is suitable. <\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Click on the <strong>Save Changes <\/strong>once you The payment appears under Everest Forms \u2192 Entries.<\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Accepting Recurring Payments (Subscriptions)<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Use this if you want to charge visitors automatically on a schedule \u2014 for example, every month or every year.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-41.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"431\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-41-1024x431.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5924\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-41-1024x431.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-41-300x126.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-41-768x323.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-41-1536x646.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-41-360x151.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-41.png 1904w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The Subscription Plan field lets visitors choose between different plans \u2014 for example, Monthly vs Annual.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>For PayPal subscription payments, add the <strong>Payment Gateway<\/strong> field to the form and ensure that <strong>PayPal<\/strong> is enabled.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Then, in the form builder, go to <strong>Fields \u2192 Payment Fields<\/strong> and drag <strong>Subscription Plan<\/strong> into your form.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Click the field to open its settings. By default it has 3 plan options \u2014 edit the label and price for each.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>To add more plans click the + button; to remove one click the x button.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>To pre-select a plan, click the circle next to that option.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>To add product images to each plan, turn on Use image choices and upload an image per plan.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Add a trial period<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can let new subscribers try your service for free before their first charge.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Click the Subscription Plan field to open its settings.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Turn on Enable Trial Period.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Set the length \u2014 days, weeks, months, or years.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Add an expiry date<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">If your subscription should stop automatically on a specific date, turn on Enable Expiry Date and pick the date from the calendar.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Finishing Up<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Existing page<\/strong> \u2014 open the page, paste the shortcode where you want the form to appear, and update the page.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>New page<\/strong> \u2014 create a new page, paste the shortcode, and publish it.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Alternatively, copy the shortcode located beside the <strong>Save<\/strong> button, then
