Once the plugins have been <strong>installed<\/strong>, <strong>activate<\/strong> them and your addon should be enabled on your website.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-8.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"434\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-8-1024x434.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5768\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-8-1024x434.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-8-300x127.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-8-768x326.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-8-1536x652.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-8-360x153.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-8.png 1900w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-code\"><code>For a detailed guide, read our documentation on <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/premium-feature-unlock\/\">how to install and activate Everest Forms Pro<\/a>. <\/code><\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Connect Stripe<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">To make sure the Stripe addon and the Payments options work perfectly on your website, we suggest you tune its settings to fit your forms better. To open the Payments settings, visit <strong>Everest Forms <strong>\u2192<\/strong> Settings <\/strong>through your admin sidebar. Next, click on the <strong>Payments<\/strong> <strong><strong>\u2192<\/strong><\/strong> <strong>Payment Method<\/strong>  section inside payments settings.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/Everest-Forms-settings-\u2039-evf-\u2014-WordPress-06-12-2026_01_31_PM.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"431\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/Everest-Forms-settings-\u2039-evf-\u2014-WordPress-06-12-2026_01_31_PM-1024x431.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5755\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/Everest-Forms-settings-\u2039-evf-\u2014-WordPress-06-12-2026_01_31_PM-1024x431.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/Everest-Forms-settings-\u2039-evf-\u2014-WordPress-06-12-2026_01_31_PM-300x126.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/Everest-Forms-settings-\u2039-evf-\u2014-WordPress-06-12-2026_01_31_PM-768x323.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/Everest-Forms-settings-\u2039-evf-\u2014-WordPress-06-12-2026_01_31_PM-1536x646.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/Everest-Forms-settings-\u2039-evf-\u2014-WordPress-06-12-2026_01_31_PM-360x151.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/Everest-Forms-settings-\u2039-evf-\u2014-WordPress-06-12-2026_01_31_PM.png 1904w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Once you\u2019ve installed the addon and activated it, you will be required to activate Stripe&#8217;s features on Everest form. To proceed with the Stripe setup, you will be required to use your Stripe account\u2019s API keys. To retrieve your <strong>API keys<\/strong>, follow these simple steps:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Open your <strong>Stripe Dashboard.<\/strong><\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Visit the <strong>API keys<\/strong> page by clicking under <strong>API keys<\/strong> under the <strong>Developer<\/strong> tab on the dashboard sidebar. (<strong>Developer \u2192 API keys.<\/strong>)<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Create and copy your <strong>API keys<\/strong> through this page. To learn more about the process involved in using API keys, you can refer to <a href=\"https:\/\/stripe.com\/docs\/keys\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noreferrer noopener nofollow\" aria-label=\"this page (opens in a new tab)\">this page<\/a> on the Stripe website. <\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-3.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"421\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-3-1024x421.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5759\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-3-1024x421.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-3-300x123.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-3-768x316.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-3-1536x631.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-3-360x148.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-3.png 1908w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Next, you can change <strong>various settings<\/strong> for Stripe to make it fit better for your website.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Now, return to your WordPress dashboard and navigate to <strong>Everest Forms &gt;<\/strong> <strong>Settings &gt;<\/strong> <strong>Payments<\/strong> <strong>&gt;<\/strong> <strong>Payment Method<\/strong>.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Under Stripe settings, you can insert the <strong>Publishable key<\/strong> and the <strong>Secret key<\/strong> to the appropriate fields. If you want to test the payment system before going live, use the Test keys that you may have received for the <strong>Stripe API.<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">If you want to test the payment system before going live, click on the <strong>Enable Test mode<\/strong> option. For the test mode, you can use the Test keys that you may have received for the Stripe API. You can learn more about the Test keys and receive Test credit cards by referring to this <a aria-label=\" (opens in a new tab)\" href=\"https:\/\/stripe.com\/docs\/testing\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noreferrer noopener nofollow\">page<\/a>.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"432\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-1024x432.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5756\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-1024x432.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-300x126.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-768x324.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-1536x647.png 1536w, 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Hit the <strong>Save Changes <\/strong>button when you complete the settings<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Add Stripe to Your Form<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Go to <strong>Everest Forms \u2192 All Forms<\/strong> and open the form you want to accept Stripe payments on (or create a new form).<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>In the form builder, open <strong>Fields \u2192 Payment Fields<\/strong>.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Drag the <strong>Payment Gateway<\/strong> field onto your form.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Click the <strong>Payment Gateway<\/strong> field to open its settings on the left.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>In the Gateways section, make sure the <strong>Stripe <\/strong>toggle is <strong>turned on<\/strong>.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-1.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"427\" 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">When a visitor selects Stripe on your form, a card entry box appears automatically \u2014 no extra setup needed.<\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Add what you&#8217;re charging for<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Set the price on a <strong>single item<\/strong>. You can customize it even further by changing its features. Choose between Field to fit your payment forms better<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-table\"><table><thead><tr><td><strong>Field<\/strong><\/td><td><strong>Use it when\u2026<\/strong><\/td><\/tr><\/thead><tbody><tr><td><strong>Single Item<\/strong><\/td><td>You&#8217;re selling one product or service at a fixed price<\/td><\/tr><tr><td><strong>Single Item (User (e.g. Small, Medium, Large)<\/td><\/tr><tr><td><strong>Checkbox<\/strong><\/td><td>Visitors can select more than one item at the same time<\/td><\/tr><tr><td><strong>Subscription always add this for multi-item forms<\/td><\/tr><\/tbody><\/table><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Accepting a One-Time Payment<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">For a simple payment form \u2014 like selling a product or accepting a donation \u2014 here&#8217;s all you need:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Add the <strong>Payment Gateway<\/strong> field and make sure <strong>Stripe <\/strong>is turned on.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Add a Single Item field and set your price.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Add a Total field so visitors can see what they&#8217;ll be charged.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Add an Email field \u2014 Stripe needs an email to send the payment receipt.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>After clicking <strong>Save<\/strong>, copy the generated shortcode and embed it in an existing or newly created page or post.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">When a visitor selects Stripe and submits the form, they enter their card details on the form and are charged immediately. You'll see the payment in your Stripe Dashboard and under Everest Forms \u2192 Entries.<\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Accepting Recurring Payments (Subscriptions)<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Use this if you want to charge visitors automatically on a schedule \u2014 for example, every month or every year.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-5.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"474\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-5-1024x474.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5761\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-5-1024x474.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-5-300x139.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-5-768x356.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-5-1536x711.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-5-360x167.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-5.png 1901w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The Subscription Plan field lets visitors choose between different plans \u2014 for example, Monthly vs Annual.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>For Stripe subscription payments, add the <strong>Payment Gateway<\/strong> field to the form and ensure that <strong>Stripe<\/strong> is enabled.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Then, in the form builder, go to <strong>Fields \u2192 Payment Fields<\/strong> and drag <strong>Subscription Plan<\/strong> into your form.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Click the field to open its settings. By default it has 3 plan options \u2014 edit the label and price for each.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>To add more plans click the + button; to remove one click the x button.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>To pre-select a plan, click the circle next to that option.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>To add product images to each plan, turn on Use image choices and upload an image per plan.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Add a trial period<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can let new subscribers try your service for free before their first charge.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Click the Subscription Plan field to open its settings.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Turn on Enable Trial Period.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Set the length \u2014 days, weeks, months, or years.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Add an expiry date<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">If your subscription should stop automatically on a specific date, turn on Enable Expiry Date and pick the date from the calendar.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Send Extra Customer Details to Stripe<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-6.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-6-1024x432.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-6-300x127.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-6-768x324.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-6-1536x648.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-6-360x152.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/06\/image-6.png 1902w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">By default, Stripe receives the payment amount and email. You can also send the visitor&#8217;s name, phone number, address, and a transaction description. This makes it easier to identify payments in your Stripe Dashboard.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Click on the Payment Gateway<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Go to <strong>Gateways \u2192 Stripe <\/strong>and click on the accordion.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Use the dropdowns to match each Stripe field (Name, Description, Phone, Address) to the matching field on your form.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Click Save.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Map a tax number<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Add a Number field to your form and label it (e.g. VAT Number).<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Go to <strong>Payments \u2192 Stripe<\/strong>, enable Sync Fields with Stripe, and on the Tax Number row select the Number field you added.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Click Save.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Finishing Up<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Click <strong>Save<\/strong> in the top right of the form builder.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Embed the form directly into a page:\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Existing page<\/strong> \u2014 open the page, paste the shortcode where you want the form to appear, and update the page.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>New page<\/strong> \u2014 create a new page, paste the shortcode, and publish it.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Alternatively, copy the shortcode located beside the <strong>Save<\/strong> button, then add a <strong>Shortcode<\/strong> block to any page and paste the shortcode into that block.<br><\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">How to setup webhook for Stripe<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Copy your Stripe webhook endpoint URL:<code>https:\/\/your-site.com\/?evf-stripe-webhook=1<\/code><\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Log in to your&nbsp;<strong>Stripe Dashboard<\/strong>&nbsp;and go to&nbsp;<strong>Developers \u2192 Webhooks<\/strong>.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Click&nbsp;<strong>Add endpoint<\/strong>&nbsp;and paste the URL above into the&nbsp;<strong>Endpoint URL<\/strong>&nbsp;field.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Under&nbsp;<strong>Select events<\/strong>, add the events below, then click&nbsp;<strong>Add endpoint<\/strong>.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Stripe generates a&nbsp;<strong>Signing secret<\/strong>&nbsp;(it starts with&nbsp;<code>whsec_<\/code>). Click&nbsp;<strong>Reveal<\/strong>&nbsp;and copy it.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>In WordPress, go to&nbsp;<strong>Everest Forms \u2192 Settings \u2192 Payments \u2192 Payment Method<\/strong>&nbsp;and expand&nbsp;<strong>Stripe Settings<\/strong>. Paste the secret into the&nbsp;<strong>Live Webhook Signing Secret<\/strong>&nbsp;field (or&nbsp;<strong>Test Webhook Signing Secret<\/strong>&nbsp;if you&#8217;re in test mode).<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Save your settings.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">Note: Use the Test signing secret while Test mode is enabled and the Live secret once you go live. The secret must match the mode, or Stripe's signature check will fail and the webhook is rejected.<\/pre>\n","protected":false},"doc_category":[61]},{"id":5588,"slug":"analytics","link":"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/analytics\/","title":{"rendered":"How to Use Analytics in Everest Forms"},"content":{"rendered":"\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The Analytics dashboard helps you track and analyze user behavior across all your Everest Forms. You can monitor submissions, view conversion rates, check device breakdowns, and identify your top-performing forms all from a single page in your WordPress dashboard.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-code\"><code>Prerequisites:\n- <a href=\"https:\/\/everestforms.net\/pricing\/\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noreferrer noopener\">Everest Forms Pro<\/a>.<\/code><\/pre>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Access the Analytics Dashboard<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Go to <strong>Everest Forms \u2192 Analytics<\/strong> in your WordPress admin. By default, the dashboard displays analytics for all forms.<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/evf-access-analytics-1.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"222\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/evf-access-analytics-1-1024x222.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5611\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/evf-access-analytics-1-1024x222.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/evf-access-analytics-1-300x65.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/evf-access-analytics-1-768x167.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/evf-access-analytics-1-1536x333.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/evf-access-analytics-1-360x78.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/evf-access-analytics-1.png 1852w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Review Key Metrics<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The top section displays summary cards with your most important form analytics. Each card also shows a comparison with the previous period so you can spot trends at a glance.You can change the metric displayed on each card using its dropdown. Available options include:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Total Submissions<\/strong>: Total number of form entries submitted.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong><strong>Complete Submissions<\/strong><\/strong>: Number of fully completed form submissions.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong><strong>Incomplete Submissions<\/strong><\/strong>: Number of partially filled forms that were not completed.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong><strong>Impressions<\/strong><\/strong>: Number of times the form was viewed by users.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong><strong>Conversion Rate<\/strong><\/strong>: Percentage of form views that resulted in a submission.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong><strong>Bounce Rate<\/strong><\/strong>: Percentage of users who left without interacting with the form.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Abandonments<\/strong>: Number of users who started filling the form but didn\u2019t submit it.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Abandonment Rate<\/strong>: Percentage of started forms that were not completed.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Revenue<\/strong>: Total income generated from form submissions.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Total Transactions<\/strong>: Number of successful payment transactions made through the form.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/evf-analytics-review-matrics.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"157\" class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><\/p>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">What is shared?<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Enabling this option will share the following information with the developers.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>admin_email &#8211; Your email<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>website_url &#8211; Your site&#8217;s URL<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>wp_version &#8211; WordPress version of your site<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>php_version &#8211; PHP version of your site<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>mysql_version &#8211; MySQL version of your site<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>server_software &#8211; Software used on your server<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>is_ssl &#8211; Is your site SSL or not?<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>is_multisite &#8211; Is your site a multi-site platform?<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>is_wp_com &#8211; Is your site hosted on wordpress.com?<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>is_wp_com_vip &#8211; Is your site hosted on WordPress.com VIP?<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>is_wp_cache &#8211; Is WP Cache enabled?<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>multi_site_count &#8211; How many subsites are there if multi-site?<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>active_theme &#8211; Name of the active theme<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>active_theme_version &#8211; Version of the active theme<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>locale &#8211; Language and regional settings of your website<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>timezone &#8211; Time Zone Detail<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>activated plugins &#8211; List of the activated plugins<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>entry count: Total number of entries submitted by the users via Everest Forms<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>forms count: Total number of forms created with the Everest Forms plugin<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">We take the privacy of our users very seriously and we only collect the data mentioned (such as PHP version, SSL or not, WordPress plugins and themes used, etc) with their explicit consent. The collected data will be used for the improvement and compatibility testing of our plugins and add-ons. This data will help us to ensure that our plugins work seamlessly with different PHP versions, different plugins, and themes. The data will not be shared with any third-party and will be kept confidential and secure. class=\"wp-block-heading\">Review Summary Cards<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">At the top of the report, you&#8217;ll see two summary cards:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ol Each option shows a label, a progress bar, and a percentage. The top option is highlighted.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/evf-radio-question-type.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"443\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/evf-radio-question-type-1024x443.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5528\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/evf-radio-question-type-1024x443.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/evf-radio-question-type-300x130.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/evf-radio-question-type-768x332.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/evf-radio-question-type-1536x664.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/evf-radio-question-type-360x156.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/evf-radio-question-type.png 1903w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\"><strong>Scale Rating<\/strong><\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Displays a score gauge with three color-coded zones red (low scores), orange (mid-range), and green (high scores). Below the gauge, you&#8217;ll see a breakdown of each segment with its percentage and count. Four metric boxes appear above the chart: <strong>Average<\/strong>, <strong>Median<\/strong>, <strong>Most Chosen<\/strong>, and <strong>Top Box<\/strong> (percentage of respondents who chose the highest values).<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/evf-scale-rating-question-type.png\"><img class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Manage your form&#8217;s security settings to protect uploaded files, ensure data privacy, and prevent spam.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">General <\/h2>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/image-10.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"288\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/image-10-1024x288.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5604\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/image-10-1024x288.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/image-10-300x84.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/image-10-768x216.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/image-10-1536x432.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/image-10-360x101.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/image-10.png 1901w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Uploaded File Protection <span class=\"premium-feature\"><strong>Pro<\/strong><\/span> <\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Enable this option to protect uploaded files from direct public access. When enabled, users cannot access uploaded files via direct URL, improving the security of sensitive data<br><\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/image-11.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"331\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/image-11-1024x331.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5606\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/image-11-1024x331.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/image-11-300x97.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/image-11-768x248.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/image-11-1536x497.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/image-11-360x116.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/image-11.png 1893w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><\/p>\n\n\n\n<pre id=\"block-b95dc5e3-090b-4ce9-ab49-c797d0e90df4\" class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">Look at <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/how-to-protect-uploaded-files-from-direct-access-pro\/\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noreferrer noopener\">How to Protect Uploaded Files from Direct Access in Everest Forms?<\/a> <\/pre>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Integration<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">With the Everest Forms plugin, you can enhance your form security using different spam protection methods. These include CAPTCHA-based solutions as well as modern CAPTCHA alternatives and background anti-spam services.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">CAPTCHA solutions help protect your forms by verifying that submissions are made by real users. In addition, some services work silently in the background to detect and block spam without requiring any user interaction, providing a smoother user experience.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/image-6.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"440\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/image-6-1024x440.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-5408\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/image-6-1024x440.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/image-6-300x129.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/image-6-768x330.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/image-6-1536x660.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/image-6-360x155.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2026\/03\/image-6.png 1873w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">There are three types of Captcha you can add to your forms. They are:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>reCAPTCHA v2 by Google<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>reCAPTCHAv3 by Google<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>hCaptcha<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">reCAPTCHA v2<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The reCaptcha v2 requires the user to click a checkbox indicating the user is not a robot. This will either pass the user immediately (with No CAPTCHA) or challenge them to validate whether or not they are human. This is the simplest option to integrate with and only requires two lines of HTML to render the checkbox.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">Look at <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/everest-forms\/docs\/how-to-integrate-google-recaptcha\/#6-toc-title\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noreferrer noopener nofollow\">How to integrate the Google reCaptcha v2 on Everest Forms?<\/a> <\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">reCAPTCHA v3<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">reCAPTCHA v3 allows you to verify if an interaction is legitimate without any user interaction. It is a pure JavaScript API returning a score, giving you the ability to take action in the context of your site: for instance requiring additional factors of authentication, sending a post to moderation, or throttling bots that may be scraping content.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">Here is <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/everest-forms\/docs\/how-to-integrate-google-recaptcha\/#4-toc-title\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noreferrer noopener nofollow\">How to integrate the Google reCaptcha v3 on Everest Forms?<\/a><\/pre>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">Note: <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/everest-forms\/docs\/how-to-integrate-google-recaptcha\/#0-toc-title\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noreferrer noopener nofollow\">How to generate the reCaptcha Site key and Secret key?<\/a><\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">hCaptcha<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The \u2018hCaptcha\u2018 is a CAPTCHA service by a company called Intuition Machines Inc which provides more reliable bot detection while being simple for humans to solve.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">Looking for <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/everest-forms\/docs\/how-to-integrate-hcaptcha\/\" rel=\"nofollow noopener\" target=\"_blank\">How to integrate the hCaptcha on Everest Forms?<\/a><\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Cloudflare<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Cloudflare&#8217;s Captcha feature uses advanced algorithms and machine learning techniques to evaluate user behavior, analyze patterns, and determine whether a visitor is likely a human or a bot. Forms Zapier API"},"content":{"rendered":"\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The Everest Forms API enables seamless integration between Everest Forms and other platforms. This REST API provides endpoints for authentication, form management, entry creation, and webhook subscriptions.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Authentication&nbsp;<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">All requests require these parameters:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><code>site_url<\/code> &#8211; Your WordPress site URL (URL encoded)<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><code>api_key<\/code> &#8211; Your API key from Everest Forms settings<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><code>everest-forms-zapier<\/code> &#8211; Always set to <code>1<\/code><\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Test Authentication:<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-code\"><code>GET https:\/\/yoursite.com?site_url=https:\/\/yoursite.com&amp;api_key=your_key&amp;everest-forms-zapier=1<\/code><\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Get Forms List<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Trigger Type:<\/strong> Polling<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Method:<\/strong> GET&nbsp;<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Endpoint:<\/strong>&nbsp;<code>https:\/\/yoursite.com?site_url={{site_url}}&amp;api_key={{api_key}}&amp;everest-forms-zapier=1&nbsp;<\/code><\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Required Parameters:<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-table\"><table class=\"has-fixed-layout\"><tbody><tr><td><strong>Parameter<\/strong><\/td><td><strong>Type<\/strong><\/td><td><strong>Required<\/strong><\/td><td><strong>Description<\/strong><\/td><\/tr><tr><td><code>site_url<\/code><\/td><td>string<\/td><td>Yes<\/td><td>Your WordPress site URL (URL encoded)<\/td><\/tr><tr><td><code>api_key<\/code><\/td><td>string<\/td><td>Yes<\/td><td>Your API key from Everest Forms settings<\/td><\/tr><tr><td><code>everest-forms-zapier<\/code><\/td><td>string<\/td><td>Yes<\/td><td>Must be set to &#8216;1&#8217;<\/td><\/tr><\/tbody><\/table><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">New Form Submission (Trigger )&nbsp;<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Action<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">1. It should be saved in your website database.&nbsp;<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">2. Perform List&nbsp;<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p &#8216;1&#8217;<\/td><\/tr><\/tbody><\/table><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Get Forms&nbsp;<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Trigger Type:<\/strong> to Let Users Download Quiz Form After Submission using Everest Forms?"},"content":{"rendered":"\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Using <strong>Everest Forms<\/strong> &#8211; a WordPress form and quiz builder plugin, you can allow users to download their WordPress quiz form with responses and scores in PDF format. This feature is handy for educational websites, training platforms, and assessment forms where users need a record of their quiz performance.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-code\"><code><strong>Prerequisites<\/strong>:\n- Active Installation of <a href=\"https:\/\/everestforms.net\/pricing\/\">Everest Forms Pro<\/a> v1.9.4 or above.\n- <a href=\"https:\/\/everestforms.net\/features\/pdf-form-submission\/\">PDF Form Submission addon<\/a> (installed and activated).\n- <a href=\"https:\/\/everestforms.net\/features\/surveys-polls-and-quiz\/\">Survey, Polls, and Quiz addon<\/a> (installed and activated).<\/code><\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Step-by-Step Configuration Guide <\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">After you&#8217;ve <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/premium-feature-unlock\/#3-toc-title\">activated Everest Forms Pro and enabled the necessary addons<\/a>, follow these steps to let users download their quiz form submission: <\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Step 1: Enable the &#8220;Download PDF after form submit&#8221; Option<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Navigate to your WordPress admin dashboard and go to <strong>Everest Forms > Settings. <\/strong>Now, click on the <strong>PDF Submission <\/strong>tab. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/navigate-to-pdf-submission.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"780\" height=\"730\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/navigate-to-pdf-submission.png\" alt=\"Navigate to PDF Submission\" class=\"wp-image-5187\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/navigate-to-pdf-submission.png 780w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/navigate-to-pdf-submission-300x281.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/navigate-to-pdf-submission-768x719.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/navigate-to-pdf-submission-360x337.png 360w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 780px) 100vw, 780px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Scroll down to locate the <strong>Download PDF after form submit <\/strong>option. Once you get the option, toggle to enable this feature. <span style=\"margin: 0px; padding: 0px;\">Below, you&#8217;ll find\u00a0<strong>Custom PDF Download Text<\/strong>, from which<\/span> you can customize the download link text. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/enable-download-pdf-after-form-submit.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"532\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/enable-download-pdf-after-form-submit-1024x532.png\" alt=\"Enable Download PDF After Form Submit\" class=\"wp-image-5193\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/enable-download-pdf-after-form-submit-1024x532.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/enable-download-pdf-after-form-submit-300x156.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/enable-download-pdf-after-form-submit-768x399.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/enable-download-pdf-after-form-submit-360x187.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/enable-download-pdf-after-form-submit.png 1384w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Click <strong>Save Changes <\/strong>to apply your settings. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Step 2: Create a New WordPress Quiz Form or Edit an Existing One<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Next, navigate to <strong>Everest Forms > Add New <\/strong>and <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/how-to-create-a-form-with-everest-forms\/\">create a new form<\/a>. If you already have an existing quiz form, you can open it to edit it. From the form field panel, drag and drop the <strong>Checkbox <\/strong>field<strong> <\/strong>into your form builder. Now, add all your quizzes with the necessary instructions for each question. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/drag-and-drop-checkboxes-field.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"463\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/drag-and-drop-checkboxes-field-1024x463.png\" alt=\"Drag-and-drop Checkboxes Field\" class=\"wp-image-5190\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/drag-and-drop-checkboxes-field-1024x463.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/drag-and-drop-checkboxes-field-300x136.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/drag-and-drop-checkboxes-field-768x348.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/drag-and-drop-checkboxes-field-1536x695.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/drag-and-drop-checkboxes-field-360x163.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/drag-and-drop-checkboxes-field.png 1810w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-code\"><code><span style=\"margin: 0px; padding: 0px;\">You can read our\u00a0<a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/survey-polls-and-quiz\/\" target=\"_blank\">documentation using the Survey, Polls, and Quiz add-on for a detailed guide<\/a><\/span>.<\/code><\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Step 3: Enable Quiz and Configure PDF Submission<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Navigate to the Settings tab of the form. appears based on your configuration.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Score Display<\/strong>: Scores are visible in the PDF if &#8220;<strong>Show Quiz Scores<\/strong>&#8221; is enabled.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>File Generation<\/strong>: A PDF is generated with quiz results and scores.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">To test the download function for quiz submission, visit your page or post in an incognito\/private window. Now, fill in the form and click on the <strong>Submit <\/strong>button. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/download-quiz-form-submission.gif\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1343\" height=\"773\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/06\/download-quiz-form-submission.gif\" alt=\"Download Quiz Form Submission\" class=\"wp-image-5195\"\/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">A message with a download link for the PDF file for your submission should appear on your screen. Click on the link, and choose the location for the file to download on your device. You can now view the response in the file. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-code\"><code>The generated PDF will include information based on your configuration. For more configuration options, read our <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/pdf-form-submission\/\">documentation on the PDF form submission<\/a>. <\/code><\/pre>\n","protected":false},"doc_category":[9]},{"id":5111,"slug":"how-to-edit-meta-key-of-the-form-fields","link":"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/how-to-edit-meta-key-of-the-form-fields\/","title":{"rendered":"How to Edit Meta Key of the Form Fields in Everest Forms?"},"content":{"rendered":"\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Everest Forms allows advanced users to edit the meta keys associated with form fields. While this feature provides greater flexibility for data management and integration with other systems, it requires careful handling to prevent data loss and form functionality issues.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-verse has-text-align-left has-white-color has-text-color has-background has-link-color wp-elements-1\" style=\"background-color:#c00f03\">\u26a0\ufe0f<strong>Important Warning<\/strong><br>Editing meta keys carries significant risks:<br>- <strong>Data Loss:<\/strong> Changing a meta key will break the connection to previously submitted data.<br><strong>- Form Malfunction:<\/strong> Improperly formatted meta keys may cause form processing errors.<br><strong>- Integration Failure<\/strong>: External connections relying on specific meta keys will stop working.<br><strong>- Conditional Logic Issues<\/strong>: Any conditional logic using the field will need reconfiguration.<\/pre>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Hence, only edit meta keys if you fully understand these consequences and have a specific technical need. Approach any modifications with extreme care, ensuring they are necessary and completed accurately.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-code\"><code>Compatibility Note: The meta key editor feature is available in Everest Forms Pro version 1.9.3 and higher.<\/code><\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Editing Meta Keys in Everest Forms: Proceed with Caution<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">For the safest experience when editing meta keys, follow this process:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Preparation Phase<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Create a complete backup<\/strong> of your WordPress database.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Go to <strong>Everest Forms<\/strong> &gt; <strong>Entries<\/strong>.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Export all entries<\/strong> for the form you&#8217;ll be modifying to CSV.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/export-form-entries-in-everest-forms.gif\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1283\" height=\"690\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/export-form-entries-in-everest-forms.gif\" alt=\"Export Form Entries in Everest Forms\" class=\"wp-image-5121\"\/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">4. Next, document the current meta keys for the form and their corresponding fields.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Editing Phase<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Log in to your WordPress admin dashboard and navigate to <strong>Everest Forms &gt; All Forms. <\/strong>Now, open the form you wish to modify.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Click on the form field you want to edit in the form builder.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>In the <strong>Field Options <\/strong>panel on the left, locate the <strong>Advanced tab. <\/strong>Here, you can find th<strong>e Meta Key<\/strong> field, which is editable.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/navigate-to-meta-key-from-field-options.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"445\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/navigate-to-meta-key-from-field-options-1024x445.png\" alt=\"Navigate to Meta Key From Field Options\" class=\"wp-image-5122\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/navigate-to-meta-key-from-field-options-1024x445.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/navigate-to-meta-key-from-field-options-300x130.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/navigate-to-meta-key-from-field-options-768x334.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/navigate-to-meta-key-from-field-options-360x157.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/navigate-to-meta-key-from-field-options.png 1380w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Note the current meta key value.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">4. Next, click on the <strong>Edit <\/strong>link, and you&#8217;ll now be allowed to edit the meta key<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">5. Edit the meta key following these formatting rules:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Begin with a letter<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Use only lowercase letters, numbers, and underscores<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Keep it concise but descriptive<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Ensure it&#8217;s unique within this form<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">6. Click on the tick to save the change, or cross out to discard the changes.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/change-meta-key.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"409\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/change-meta-key-1024x409.png\" alt=\"Change Meta Key\" class=\"wp-image-5123\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/change-meta-key-1024x409.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/change-meta-key-300x120.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/change-meta-key-768x307.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/change-meta-key-360x144.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/change-meta-key.png 1373w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">7. Repeat for any other fields that need modification.<br>8. Save the changes for the entire form after you&#8217;re done with the modification.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Now, if you go back to check the form entries, you&#8217;ll not see the data for the modified field:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/check-form-entries-after-changing-meta-key.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"546\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/check-form-entries-after-changing-meta-key-1024x546.png\" alt=\"Check Form Entries After Changing Meta Key\" class=\"wp-image-5125\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/check-form-entries-after-changing-meta-key-1024x546.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/check-form-entries-after-changing-meta-key-300x160.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/check-form-entries-after-changing-meta-key-768x410.png 768w, class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">However, if users fill out the form after you change the meta key, the entries will have the new data.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/new-form-entry-after-editing-meta-key.png\"><img https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/new-form-entry-after-editing-meta-key-768x406.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/new-form-entry-after-editing-meta-key-360x191.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/05\/new-form-entry-after-editing-meta-key.png 1266w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Testing Phase<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">To make sure that your form is working correctly:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Create a test submission.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Verify that the submission appears correctly in Entries.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Check that any integrations or conditional logic still function.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Validate any custom code that references these fields.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Common Issues and Troubleshooting<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Missing previous entry data<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>This is expected behavior. Changing a meta key creates a new database column, disconnecting from previous submissions.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Use your exported data to reference historical submissions.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Form submission errors<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Verify your meta keys follow the formatting guidelines.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Check for duplicate meta keys within the form.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Ensure meta keys aren&#8217;t excessively long.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Integration failures<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Update any integration mappings to use the new meta keys.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Reconfigure webhooks or API connections with updated field references.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Hence, strictly follow the steps mentioned in the preparation phase before editing the meta key for any field.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Fields That Do Not Support Meta Key Editing<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Certain field types in Everest Forms do not allow for meta key editing. These include:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Custom HTML: <\/strong>This field type allows you to add custom HTML codes to your form but does not grant you the ability to edit its meta key. <\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Divider: <\/strong>The Divider field type creates visual separations between different sections in your form. However, you cannot edit its meta key.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Section Title: <\/strong>This field type adds titles to your sections for better organization and aesthetics. Like the above, this field type does not provide a feature for meta key editing. <\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Reset:<\/strong> The Reset field type allows users to clear all input data in the form. Despite its practicality, it does not include the option for meta key editing.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">We recommend creating new fields if meta key editing is crucial for your form.<\/p>\n","protected":false},"doc_category":[5]},{"id":5098,"slug":"cleantalk","link":"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/cleantalk\/","title":{"rendered":"CleanTalk"},"content":{"rendered":"\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Everest Forms<\/strong> now integrates with<strong> CleanTalk<\/strong>, a powerful anti-spam solution that helps protect your WordPress forms from spam submissions, bot attacks, and other threats.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-code\"><code>Prerequisite:\n- Active <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/installing-a-plugin\/\">installation of the Everest Forms plugin<\/a>.\n- Access Key from the CleanTalk (free registration available).<\/code><\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Setting Up CleanTalk Integration Using Everest Forms<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Follow these simple steps to use CleanTalk&#8217;s anti-spam services directly within Everest Forms:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Step 1: Access CleanTalk Settings<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Log in to <span style=\"margin: 0px; While some of the anti-spam tools are available for free, few of them are available in <strong>Everest Forms Pro<\/strong>.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"has-background wp-block-paragraph\" style=\"background-color:#97d0d01f\"><strong>Prerequisite:<\/strong><br>\u2611\ufe0f <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs-category\/getting-started\/\">Active Installation of Everest Forms Free or Pro Plugin<\/a><\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">How to Prevent WordPress Contact Form Spam?<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Option 1: Form Restriction <span class=\"premium-feature\"><strong>Pro<\/strong><\/span><\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Form restriction<\/strong> is a premium addon, available with Everest Forms Pro. It allows you to restrict form submission based on the multiple conditions you set. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">When to use Form Restriction Addon?<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">It&#8217;s a&nbsp;<strong>first-line defense<\/strong>&nbsp;against spam, acting as a gatekeeper to minimize unnecessary exposure. By reducing the form&#8217;s exposure to untrusted sources before you enable other anti-spam tools, you can address the problem at its root using this addon.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/form-restriction-addon.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"595\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/form-restriction-addon-1024x595.png\" alt=\"Form Restriction Addon\" class=\"wp-image-4802\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/form-restriction-addon-1024x595.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/form-restriction-addon-300x174.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/form-restriction-addon-768x446.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/form-restriction-addon-360x209.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/form-restriction-addon.png 1176w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Features available:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Entry limitation (e.g., 100 submissions\/form).<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Form scheduling to let users submit the form at a specific time<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>User restriction<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Password restriction to ensure only authorized individuals submit the form.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Form submission<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Form restriction by country to restrict specific regions<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">How to stop spam on WordPress contact forms using Form Restriction Addon?<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">To use the feature, you need to activate the <strong><a href=\"https:\/\/everestforms.net\/features\/form-restriction\/\">Form Restriction<\/a><\/strong> addon (Requires Everest Forms Pro).<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"has-background wp-block-paragraph\" style=\"background-color:#97d0d01f\">Here&#8217;s a complete guide on <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/form-restriction\/\">using Form Restriction<\/a>.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Option 2: Using Honeypot (Free)<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Honeypot is a spam prevention technique that traps bots using a hidden field that is invisible to users. If filled, submissions are automatically rejected as spam. The form\u2019s appearance remains unchanged for humans.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">When to use Honeypot on Forms?<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>When you want to block simple bots that fill out your forms automatically.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">How to stop spam on WordPress contact forms using Honeypot?<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Navigate to the form where you want to add anti-spam and security settings. From your <strong>Anti-Spam and Security <\/strong>menu, toggle&nbsp;<strong>Enable Anti-Spam Honeypot<\/strong>&nbsp;in your form.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/enable-honeypot.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"319\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/enable-honeypot-1024x319.png\" alt=\"Enable Honeypot\" class=\"wp-image-4813\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/enable-honeypot-1024x319.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/enable-honeypot-300x94.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/enable-honeypot-768x239.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/enable-honeypot-360x112.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/enable-honeypot.png 1363w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Hit the <strong>Save <\/strong>button to activate the honeypot. Humans will see the form without the hidden field, but bots detect and fill it, causing automatic rejection. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Option 3: Add Waiting Time for Form Submission (Free)<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">This option enables a minimum waiting time for the users to submit the form and hence blocks instant bot submissions (e.g., 20 seconds).<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">When to use Waiting time for form submission?<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>When you&#8217;ve enabled honeypot or form restriction, but the bots are rapidly attacking your form, you can enable this option.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">How to enable Waiting time for form submission?<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">On your <strong>Anti-Spam and Security <\/strong>interface of the form, toggle&nbsp;<strong>Enable Minimum Waiting Time<\/strong>&nbsp;and set the duration. Sets a minimum time (e.g., 20 seconds) before users can submit the form, blocking instant bot submissions.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/enable-waiting-time-for-form-submission.png\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/02\/enable-waiting-time-for-form-submission.png\" alt=\"Enable Waiting Time for Form Submission\"\/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Once the user fills in the form and clicks on the submit button, the waiting time message is shown:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/02\/waiting-time-for-form-submission.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"722\" height=\"640\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/02\/waiting-time-for-form-submission.png\" alt=\"Waiting Time for Form Submission\" class=\"wp-image-4865\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/02\/waiting-time-for-form-submission.png 722w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/02\/waiting-time-for-form-submission-300x266.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/02\/waiting-time-for-form-submission-360x319.png 360w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 722px) 100vw, 722px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Note: Enabling Honeypot and adding waiting time requires no user interaction, and hence, these anti-spam methods work silently in the background.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Option 4: CAPTCHA Integration (Free)<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can also add visual\/spam checks via third-party services. Everest Forms is the best captcha plugin for WordPress and supports a wide range of <a href=\"https:\/\/everestforms.net\/features\/security\/\"><strong>CAPTCHA<\/strong><\/a> types: <strong>reCaptcha<\/strong>, <strong>hCaptcha<\/strong>, or <strong>Turnstile<\/strong>. All of them are different types of bot protection mechanisms used to verify that a user is human before allowing form submission<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">When to use CAPTCHA for spam protection?<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>If the bots are bypassing simple anti-spam techniques like honeypot and waiting time, you can enable any one of these CAPTCHA options.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">How to stop spam on WordPress contact forms using CAPTCHA?<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Setup:<\/strong> Navigate to <strong>Everest Forms &gt; Settings &gt; Captcha<\/strong>. From here, you\u2019ll get all the available CAPTCHA options:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Select your preferred Captcha service and enter your Site and Secret Keys.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/captcha-settings-in-everest-forms.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"500\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/captcha-settings-in-everest-forms-1024x500.png\" alt=\"Captcha Settings in Everest Forms\" class=\"wp-image-4797\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/captcha-settings-in-everest-forms-1024x500.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/captcha-settings-in-everest-forms-300x147.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/captcha-settings-in-everest-forms-768x375.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/captcha-settings-in-everest-forms-1536x751.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/captcha-settings-in-everest-forms-360x176.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/captcha-settings-in-everest-forms.png 1596w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Save the changes when you&#8217;re done. Meanwhile, you can only select a single CAPTCHA type. Also, the <strong>Site Key <\/strong>and <strong>Secret Key <\/strong>are available through their respective website.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Detailed Setup Guides:<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul style=\"background-color:#97d0d01f\" class=\"wp-block-list has-background\">\n<li><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/how-to-integrate-google-recaptcha\/\">reCaptcha Setup<\/a><\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/how-to-integrate-hcaptcha\/\">hCaptcha Setup<\/a> &nbsp;<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/how-to-integrate-cloudflare-turnstile-with-the-everest-forms\/\">Turnstile Setup<\/a> &nbsp;<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/how-to-integrate-shield-silentcaptcha-with-everest-forms\/\" data-type=\"link\" data-id=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/how-to-integrate-shield-silentcaptcha-with-everest-forms\/\">Shield silentCAPTCHA<\/a><\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Option 5: Custom CAPTCHA Integration <span class=\"premium-feature\"><strong>Pro<\/strong><\/span><\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">If you want to add an extra layer of security to your form, you can add a custom Captcha to your forms. This allows you to add custom challenges (e.g., math questions) prepared by yourself that can only be solved by humans.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">When to use CAPTCHA Addon?<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>To add a personalized layer of security to your WordPress forms<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">How to use CAPTCHA Addon in Everest Forms?<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Setup:<\/strong> You must activate<strong> Everest Forms Pro<\/strong> and enable the&nbsp;<strong><a href=\"https:\/\/everestforms.net\/features\/custom-captcha\/\">Custom CAPTCHA<\/a><\/strong>&nbsp;addon. Once you get the addon, you can add the captcha field to your form.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/enable-custom-captcha.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"998\" height=\"706\" class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Simply open the form where you want to add a custom captcha, drag and drop the Captcha field, and set custom questions.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image 1438w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">When the user fills out the form, he\/she will also have to answer the math question before submitting it.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"has-background wp-block-paragraph\" style=\"background-color:#97d0d01f\">For a detailed guide, read our documentation on <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/captcha\/\">Custom CAPTCHA<\/a>.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Option 6: Using the Spam Protection Plugin Akismet (Free)<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Akismet is an anti-spam plugin that blocks spam contact form submissions by checking them against a global spam database. While CAPTCHA-based solutions prevent bots from submitting forms, <strong>Akismet actively filters out unwanted spam<\/strong>, including those submitted by real humans.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">When to use the Akismet plugin?<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Instead of manually reviewing flagged submissions, Akismet <strong>automatically marks spam<\/strong> and lets you review only if needed. This is especially helpful for high-traffic sites with lots of user-generated content.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">How to stop spam on WordPress contact forms using the Akismet plugin?<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Setup: <\/strong>It requires&nbsp;you to install and activate the <a href=\"https:\/\/wordpress.org\/plugins\/akismet\/\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noreferrer noopener nofollow\">Akismet plugin<\/a> to use this spam protection option. Once the plugin is installed, enable Akismet through <strong>Anti-Spam and Security <\/strong>settings.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/akismet-plugin.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"255\" 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/akismet-plugin-768x191.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/akismet-plugin-360x90.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/akismet-plugin.png 1447w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can choose the protection type between: <strong>Make the form submission as failed<\/strong> or <strong>Mark as spam<\/strong>.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/02\/akismet-spam-protection-type.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"672\"
