If you're using the legacy Payments tab, continue with this documentation. Otherwise, if you're using the new <strong>Payment Gateway<\/strong> field, please refer to our <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/authorize-via-payment-gateway\/\">Payment Gateway documentation<\/a>.<\/pre>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">If you want to make payments easier on your website, the Authorize.Net add-on for Everest Forms is a great solution. With this tool, your users can securely pay with their credit cards without leaving your site. It&#8217;s perfect for both one-time payments and regular subscriptions. In this guide, we&#8217;ll show you how to use it to improve your online business.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 id=\"installation\" class=\"wp-block-heading\">Installation<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Firstly, you need to purchase the Everest Forms Pro plugin.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>After the successful purchase, you will get the plugin zip file under your WPEverest account page.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Now, you can download the Everest forms pro plugin and <strong>Authorize.net<\/strong> add-on from there.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Through your WordPress Dashboard, visit&nbsp;<strong>Plugins -&gt; Add New -&gt; Upload<\/strong>&nbsp;and then choose the downloaded zip file and then install and activate it.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Now you are ready to use this add-on for your site.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Alternative Method:<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Once you have entered the Everest Forms Pro license on your site, go to <strong>Everest Forms &gt; Add-ons<\/strong>.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Search for the &#8216;<strong>Authorize.net<\/strong>&#8216; add-on.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Click on &#8216;<strong>Install<\/strong>&#8216;.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Once the installation is finished, click on &#8216;<strong>Activate<\/strong>&#8216;. This helps ensure that legitimate users can access the website seamlessly while blocking or challenging suspicious or malicious traffic.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Generating Cloudflare Keys<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Log in or create an account on Cloudflare <a href=\"https:\/\/dash.cloudflare.com\/login\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noreferrer noopener nofollow\">from here<\/a>.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Once you\u2019ve logged in, Go to <strong> Recent  <\/strong>>>\u00a0<strong>Turnstile<\/strong>\u00a0menu and click on\u00a0<strong>Add widget Manually<\/strong><\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/06\/Turnstile-setting.png\"><img This document provides an overview of the available options in the plugin&#8217;s free and premium versions.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"has-background wp-block-paragraph\" style=\"background-color:#97d0d01f\">This document provides a general overview of Everest Forms anti-spam features. based on your needs. Let&#8217;s get into the multiple possibilities you get with the Everest Forms plugin to prevent form spam.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Anti-Spam and Security Options in Everest Forms<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Option 1: Honeypot <span class=\"premium-feature\">FREE<\/span><\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Honeypot is a hidden field technique that catches automated submissions while remaining invisible to human users. Bots that fill this field are automatically rejected without disrupting the user experience.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">From your <strong>Anti-Spam and Security <\/strong>menu, toggle&nbsp;<strong>Enable Anti-Spam Honeypot<\/strong>&nbsp;in your form.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/enable-honeypot.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"319\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/enable-honeypot-1024x319.png\" alt=\"Enable Honeypot\" class=\"wp-image-4813\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/enable-honeypot-1024x319.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/enable-honeypot-300x94.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/enable-honeypot-768x239.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/enable-honeypot-360x112.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/enable-honeypot.png 1363w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Option 2: Add Waiting Time for Form Submission <span class=\"premium-feature\">FREE<\/span><\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Establishes a required delay between form loading and submission to prevent instantaneous bot submissions. You can configure the waiting period (e.g., 20 seconds) to block automated form-filling. <span style=\"margin: 0px; padding: 0px;\">Toggle&nbsp;<strong>Enable Minimum Waiting Time<\/strong>&nbsp;and set the duration on your <strong>Anti-Spam and Security<\/strong> interface of the form<\/span>. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/enable-waiting-time-for-form-submission.png\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/02\/enable-waiting-time-for-form-submission.png\" alt=\"Enable Waiting Time for Form Submission\"\/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Option 3: CAPTCHA Integration <span class=\"premium-feature\">FREE<\/span><\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Everest Forms supports multiple third-party <a href=\"https:\/\/everestforms.net\/features\/security\/\"><strong>CAPTCHA<\/strong><\/a> types: <strong>reCaptcha<\/strong>, <strong>hCaptcha<\/strong>, or <strong>Turnstile<\/strong>. Configuration is available under <strong>Everest Forms &gt; Settings &gt; CAPTCHA<\/strong>, where you must enter your site and secret keys from your chosen CAPTCHA provider.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure humans can reasonably solve. This option requires the <strong><a href=\"https:\/\/everestforms.net\/features\/custom-captcha\/\">Custom CAPTCHA<\/a><\/strong>&nbsp;addon that comes with Everest Forms Pro. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/enable-custom-captcha.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"998\" height=\"706\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/enable-custom-captcha.png\" alt=\"Enable Custom Captcha\" class=\"wp-image-4800\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/enable-custom-captcha.png 998w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/enable-custom-captcha-300x212.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/enable-custom-captcha-768x543.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/enable-custom-captcha-360x255.png 360w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 998px) 100vw, 998px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"has-background wp-block-paragraph\" style=\"background-color:#97d0d01f\">Here&#8217;s our detailed guide on using <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/captcha\/\">Custom CAPTCHA<\/a>.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Option 5: Spam Protection Plugin &#8211; Akismet <span class=\"premium-feature\">FREE<\/span><\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Akismet is an anti-spam plugin that checks submissions against a global spam database. To use the options, you must install the <a href=\"https:\/\/wordpress.org\/plugins\/akismet\/\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noreferrer noopener nofollow\">Akismet plugin<\/a> on your WordPress site. Once the plugin is installed, enable Akismet through <strong>Anti-Spam and Security <\/strong>settings.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/akismet-plugin.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"255\" 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/akismet-plugin-768x191.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/akismet-plugin-360x90.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/akismet-plugin.png 1447w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can choose the protection type between: <strong>Make the form submission as failed<\/strong> or <strong>Mark as spam<\/strong>.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/02\/akismet-spam-protection-type.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"672\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/02\/akismet-spam-protection-type.png 672w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/02\/akismet-spam-protection-type-300x129.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/02\/akismet-spam-protection-type-360x154.png 360w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 672px) 100vw, 672px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">When a user submits the form, the plugin analyzes the data for spam. If marked safe, the form is submitted; otherwise, it&#8217;s rejected or flagged as spam.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Option 6: Admin Approval Entries <span class=\"premium-feature\"><strong>Pro<\/strong><\/span><\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><a href=\"https:\/\/everestforms.net\/features\/admin-approval-entries\/\">Admin Approval Entries<\/a> lets you add an approval step where administrators receive notification of new submissions and can approve or deny entries before they&#8217;re processed fully. Navigate to <strong>Everest Forms &gt; Settings &gt; General<\/strong> and toggle<strong> Enable admin approval entries.<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/enable-admin-approval.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"402\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/enable-admin-approval-1024x402.png\" alt=\"Enable Admin Approval\" class=\"wp-image-4799\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/enable-admin-approval-1024x402.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/enable-admin-approval-300x118.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/enable-admin-approval-768x302.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/enable-admin-approval-1536x603.png 1536w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/enable-admin-approval-360x141.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/enable-admin-approval.png 1568w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"has-background wp-block-paragraph\" style=\"background-color:#97d0d01f\">Here&#8217;s a detailed guide on <a href=\"For%20more,%20here\u2019s%20our%20detailed%20guide%20https:\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/global-general-settings\/#3-toc-title\">enabling admin approval entries.<\/a><\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Option 7: Blacklist Words <span class=\"premium-feature\"><strong>Pro<\/strong><\/span><\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Reject submissions containing specific keywords or phrases. Enter comma-separated terms to block submissions containing those words under your <strong>Anti-Spam and Security<\/strong> <strong>&gt;<\/strong>&nbsp;<strong>Blacklisted Words<\/strong>&nbsp;field.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/black-listed-word.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"301\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/black-listed-word-1024x301.png\" alt=\"Black Listed Word\" class=\"wp-image-4795\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/black-listed-word-1024x301.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/black-listed-word-300x88.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/black-listed-word-768x226.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/black-listed-word-360x106.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/01\/black-listed-word.png 1447w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Option 8: Block IP and Email <span class=\"premium-feature\"><strong>Pro<\/strong><\/span><\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">This option prevents submissions from specific IP addresses and email addresses.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>IP Block:<\/strong> List out all the IP addresses you want to block. The user with these addresses won\u2019t be able to submit the form.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Email Black List<\/strong>: Similar to the IP addresses, you can list the email addresses to restrict them from submitting the form.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can add IPs\/emails to their respective fields under&nbsp;the <strong>Anti-Spam and Security<\/strong> interface to block them.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a We value your input and feedback and appreciate any suggestions you may have for improving the plugin. Our goal is to create a user-friendly and customizable registration experience for your website or application. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Steps to submit feature ideas\/requests<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">You don't have to be logged in to submit the idea. <\/pre>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Go to the <a href=\"https:\/\/wpeverest.com\/wordpress-plugins\/everest-forms\/feature-request\/\" data-type=\"URL\" data-id=\"https:\/\/wpeverest.com\/wordpress-plugins\/everest-forms\/feature-request\/\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noreferrer noopener nofollow\">Everest Forms Feature Requests Submission Page<\/a>.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/user-registration\/wp-content\/uploads\/sites\/2\/2023\/02\/image-12.png\" rel=\"nofollow noopener\" target=\"_blank\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/user-registration\/wp-content\/uploads\/sites\/2\/2023\/02\/image-12-1024x448.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-3195\"\/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Here, you will find two sections. They are:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Add Idea<\/strong><\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>View Ideas<\/strong><\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Add Idea\/Request<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">On the left side, you will find the option to submit a new idea\/request. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/user-registration\/wp-content\/uploads\/sites\/2\/2023\/02\/image-15.png\" rel=\"nofollow noopener\" target=\"_blank\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/user-registration\/wp-content\/uploads\/sites\/2\/2023\/02\/image-15.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-3203\"\/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Title: <\/strong>Add a brief title of your idea\/request.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Description: <\/strong>Explain the idea\/request you have in detail in this box.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Attach Images: <\/strong>Upload images that can be viewed as a reference to your idea.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Name: <\/strong>If you aren&#8217;t logged in, add your name here.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Email: <\/strong>Add your email address.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">After the above steps, agree on the privacy policy and terms and click on the &#8216;<strong>Add idea&#8217; <\/strong>button to submit your idea\/request. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">View Ideas\/Requests<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">On the right side of the screen, you will find a section with the ideas\/requests archive. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/user-registration\/wp-content\/uploads\/sites\/2\/2023\/02\/Idea-Lists.gif\" rel=\"nofollow noopener\" target=\"_blank\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/user-registration\/wp-content\/uploads\/sites\/2\/2023\/02\/Idea-Lists.gif\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-3197\"\/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can perform the following actions in this section.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Search: <\/strong>Since there are many users submitting ideas, the idea you have might already be listed in this section. You can search using relative keywords from the search bar on the top right of the screen.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Order: <\/strong>You can order the ideas by clicking this dropdown menu. The ideas can be ordered as trending, most votes(upvoted), most discussed, or recent.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Status: <\/strong>You can also list out the ideas based on their status. Status can be All, Not done, Not set, Done, etc.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>My ideas: <\/strong>If you click on this option, you will see all the ideas that have been submitted by you only.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li><strong>Upvote: <\/strong>On each idea, you will see a ^ button. Click on this to upvote the idea. This is one of the best options to vote for feature requests that are already listed by other users. The ideas that have the maximum upvotes are most likely to be included in future versions of the plugin.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">View Individual Idea\/Request<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">If you click on the title of any idea, you will be able to see the details of the idea. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/user-registration\/wp-content\/uploads\/sites\/2\/2023\/02\/image-14.png\" rel=\"nofollow noopener\" target=\"_blank\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/user-registration\/wp-content\/uploads\/sites\/2\/2023\/02\/image-14.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-3198\"\/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">On this page, you can comment on the idea, upvote the idea, see the voters&#8217; names, and can also check the reference images. <\/p>\n","protected":false},"doc_category":[9]},{"id":2509,"slug":"how-to-configure-caching-plugins","link":"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/how-to-configure-caching-plugins\/","title":{"rendered":"How to configure Caching plugins?"},"content":{"rendered":"\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">When using caching plugins, the below-mentioned page is frequently already excluded. Otherwise, ensure that this page is excluded from the cache via your caching system&#8217;s settings.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Contact Form Page<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Below, you will find the instruction to remove these pages from the cache for a few top caching plugins.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">WP Rocket<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The Everest Forms plugin is compatible with the WP Rocket. Please make sure that the above-mentioned pages are excluded from the cache.<br>For this, navigate to <strong>Dashboard -&gt; Pages -&gt; Contact Form<\/strong> -&gt; Check on Never cache this page on the WP Rocket Sidebar Menu.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">Here, the Contact Form page is the page with the Everest Forms form shortcode. <\/pre>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"460\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/08\/Contact-Forms-WPRocket-1024x460.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-3084\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/08\/Contact-Forms-WPRocket-1024x460.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/08\/Contact-Forms-WPRocket-300x135.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/08\/Contact-Forms-WPRocket-768x345.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/08\/Contact-Forms-WPRocket-360x162.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2023\/08\/Contact-Forms-WPRocket.png 1352w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">We suggest you avoid JS file minification from the <strong>WP Rocket -&gt; Settings -&gt; File Optimization<\/strong>.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large is-resized\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/user-registration\/wp-content\/uploads\/sites\/2\/2022\/12\/image-8-1024x456.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2963\" style=\"width:1024px;height:456px\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can also use an alternative method for avoiding the desired pages from the cache. This method also helps you to add bulk URLs for multiple pages. For this, go to <strong>Settings -&gt; WP Rocket -&gt; Advanced Rules<\/strong>. Add the desired page URLs on the <strong>Never Cache URLs<\/strong> input option.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/user-registration\/wp-content\/uploads\/sites\/2\/2022\/12\/image-9-1024x445.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2964\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">WP Super Cache<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The Everest Forms is compatible with WP Super Cache. Please make sure that the above-mentioned pages are excluded from the cache by placing the corresponding endpoints here <strong>WP Admin -&gt; Settings -&gt; WP Super Cache -&gt; Advanced<\/strong>.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"857\" height=\"253\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/12\/image-2-1.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2512\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/12\/image-2-1.png 857w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/12\/image-2-1-300x89.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/12\/image-2-1-768x227.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/12\/image-2-1-360x106.png 360w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 857px) 100vw, 857px\" \/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Excluding Everest Forms Session From The Cache<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">If the caching system you\u2019re using offers database caching, it might be helpful to exclude everest_forms_sessions from being cached. This will be dependent on the plugin or host caching so refer to the specific instructions or docs for that system.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Nonce Error<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">If you are using a caching plugin, in most cases the caching plugin generates static HTML pages of your website and saves them on your server. Each time a user tries to access your website, your caching plugin serves up the lighter HTML page instead of processing the comparatively heavier WordPress PHP scripts. Due to this, the nonce is expired, and the error is being shown. Our suggestion would be to turn off the caching for a specific page(where the form is), to solve the issue. <\/p>\n","protected":false},"doc_category":[9]},{"id":2467,"slug":"check-sites-php-and-wordpress-versions","link":"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/check-sites-php-and-wordpress-versions\/","title":{"rendered":"How to check your site&#8217;s PHP and WordPress versions?"},"content":{"rendered":"\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Sometimes, when you are trying to troubleshoot some issues, the version of your <strong>site&#8217;s PHP<\/strong> plays a significant role. On July 1, 2023, standard Universal Analytics properties will stop processing new hits. If you still rely on Universal Analytics, we recommend that you prepare to use Google Analytics 4 going forward.<\/em><\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Setting up Google Analytics 4<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Create an account from <a href=\"https:\/\/analytics.google.com\/analytics\/web\/provision\/#\/provision\/create\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noreferrer noopener nofollow\">here<\/a>. <\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/10\/GA4-Account-Setup-1-1024x942.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2317\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Add the account name and click on <strong>Next<\/strong>.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>In the <strong>Property Setup <\/strong>section, give the property name, location, and currency.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/10\/Property-SetUp-GA4-1024x379.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2318\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>You can skip the Business part.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Click on <strong>Create. <\/strong>Accept the terms and conditions and click on <strong>I Accept.<\/strong><\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/10\/GA4-Account-Creation-1024x494.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2319\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Your Google Analytics 4 account is now created under the chosen name. After this, you will be redirected to the Data Streams dashboard. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/10\/GA4-Platform-1024x497.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2322\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Choose\/Click on <strong>Web<\/strong>.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>On the next window, add your site&#8217;s URL and a stream name of your choice. <\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Click on <strong>Create Stream.<\/strong><\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/10\/Data-Stream-GA4-1-1024x491.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2323\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>After the stream is created, copy the <strong>Measurement ID <\/strong>into a clipboard.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/10\/GA4-Measurement-1-3-1024x337.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2325\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Google Tag Manager Configuration<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Now, we will need a trigger, and tracking event and then connect these through Google Tag Manager to Google Analytics 4. We present you with a new, quick, and modern way of presenting the forms. With one question at a time approach, feel more human while filling out the forms with the help of the Everest Forms &#8211; Conversation Forms add-on. Create your own disturbance-free whole-page conversational form layout with a minimum of time and effort input. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"installation\">Installation<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\"><li>Firstly, you need to purchase the Everest Forms Pro plugin.<\/li><li>After the successful purchase, you will get the plugin zip file under your WPEverest account page.<\/li><li>Now, you can download the Everest forms pro plugin and <strong>Conversational Forms<\/strong> add-on from there.<\/li><li>Through your WordPress Dashboard, visit&nbsp;<strong>Plugins -&gt; Add New -&gt; Upload<\/strong>&nbsp;and then choose the downloaded zip file and then install and activate it.<\/li><li>Now you are ready to use this add-on for your site.<\/li><\/ol>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Alternative Method:<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\"><li>Once you have entered the Everest Forms Pro license on your site, go to <strong>Everest Forms &gt; Add-ons<\/strong>.<\/li><li>Search for the &#8216;<strong><strong>Conversational Forms<\/strong><\/strong>&#8216; add-on.<\/li><li>Click on &#8216;<strong>Install<\/strong>&#8216;.<\/li><li>Once the installation is finished, click on &#8216;<strong>Activate<\/strong>&#8216;. The add-on will be activated.<\/li><\/ol>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Setup and Configuration<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Once you have finished installing the add-on, you can create a new form or edit the existing one to make it a conversational form. For this, go to the form editor and add the required form fields. This has:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\"><li><strong>Welcome Message<\/strong>: Enable this and write the desired welcome message on the title box. You can also write a description message. It will appear at the beginning of the form. <\/li><\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full is-resized\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/10\/image.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2305\" width=\"452\" height=\"156\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\"><li><strong>Button Text: <\/strong>Write the desired text to appear on the Start button.<\/li><li><strong>Form Themes: <\/strong>Choose any one of the five available themes for the form&#8217;s layout. These themes basically change the color of the form as per the indication.<\/li><\/ul>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Background Type<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\"><li><strong>Image: <\/strong>Upload or choose any image to appear on the background of the form.<\/li><li><strong>Layout: <\/strong>This decides how you want the chosen image to appear on the form.<\/li><li><strong>Opacity: <\/strong>As the name suggests, choose the opacity of the background image from here.<\/li><li><strong>Color<\/strong>: You can choose a color background instead of the image from this option. Requirements for Everest Forms"},"content":{"rendered":"\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">General Configuration Recommendations<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>WP version: 5.0 or higher<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>PHP version: 7.0 or higher<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>MySQL version: 5.6 or higher<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">PHP Configuration Requirements<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Post Max Size: 64 MB<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Memory Limit: 256 MB<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Time Limit: 300ms<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Max Upload Size: 64 MB<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>WP Remote Get: enabled<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">PHP version lesser than 7.2 is NOT SUPPORTED<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Effective immediately, we will no longer be providing support for the Everest Forms plugin on websites that are running on PHP versions older than 7.2, starting from Everest Forms version 2.0.0. We urge all users to upgrade their PHP version to ensure that they can continue to use the latest versions of our plugin without any issues.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">It is essential to note that upgrading to a supported version of PHP is crucial for your website&#8217;s security and performance. Therefore, we strongly recommend that you upgrade your PHP version at your earliest convenience to avoid any potential security risks.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Thank you for choosing our plugin, and we look forward to providing you with the best user experience possible.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Where to find the PHP version of your site?<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/user-registration\/wp-content\/uploads\/sites\/2\/2023\/05\/image.png\" rel=\"nofollow noopener\" target=\"_blank\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.wpeverest.com\/user-registration\/wp-content\/uploads\/sites\/2\/2023\/05\/image-598x1024.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-3245\"\/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Navigate to <strong>Dashboard &gt; Tools &gt; Site Health. <\/strong><\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Click on the<strong> Info<\/strong> tab.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Click on the <strong>Server<\/strong> accordion.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Here, you will find the version number of the PHP used on your site just like the above image.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">How to change the PHP version with cPanel?<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">To change the version of PHP used by your cPanel account, follow these steps:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Log in to your cPanel account.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Look for the &#8220;Select PHP Version&#8221; option in the &#8220;Software&#8221; section of cPanel.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Click on &#8220;Select PHP Version&#8221;.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>You will see a list of PHP versions that are available to you. Also, add the &#8216;<strong>Client Secret Value<\/strong>&#8216; copied from the Azure account.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/09\/Access-Code-1024x504.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2094\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\"><li>Click on &#8216;<strong>Authenticate with Microsoft Account&#8217;. <\/strong>And you are done with the connection of the Everest Forms plugin and One Drive. General<\/strong>. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/09\/OneDrive-Send-Files-1024x513.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2095\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\"><li>Select the &#8216;<strong>OneDrive File path<\/strong>&#8216;. <\/li><li>Give a path.<\/li><li>If you want to send the form as a PDF to the Amazon S3, select &#8216;<strong>Send PDF To OneDrive<\/strong>&#8216;.<\/li><\/ul>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">Note: You will have to install and activate the PDF Submission add-on for sending the PDF to OneDrive<\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Amazon S3<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">For connecting to the Amazon S3, you will need <strong>AWS Access Key ID &amp; Secret Key<\/strong>. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Getting AWS Access Key ID and Secret Key<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\"><li>Go to&nbsp;<strong><a href=\"https:\/\/console.aws.amazon.com\/\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noreferrer noopener nofollow\">Amazon Web Services<\/a><\/strong>&nbsp;console.<\/li><li>Choose &#8216;I am User&#8217; and log in with the respective credentials.<\/li><\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/09\/I-am-User-AWS.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2054\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/09\/search-bar-Iam-AWS-1024x501.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2055\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\"><li>On the search bar, type &#8216;Iam&#8217; and click on the first option.<\/li><\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/09\/Users-Section-AWS-1-1024x311.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2058\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\"><li>Under <strong>Access Management<\/strong>, click on &#8216;<strong>Users<\/strong>&#8216;.<\/li><\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/09\/Add-User-AWS-1024x495.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2059\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\"><li>Click on &#8216;<strong>Add users<\/strong>&#8216;.<\/li><\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/09\/New-User-Details-AWS-1024x493.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2060\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\"><li>Provide a username.<\/li><li>On the &#8216;<strong>Select AWS credential type<\/strong>&#8216; option, select &#8216;<strong>Access Keys-Programmatic access<\/strong>&#8216;.<\/li><\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/09\/IAM-Management-Console-AWS-1024x495.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2061\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\"><li>On the &#8216;<strong>Set Permission&#8217; <\/strong>option, select<strong> &#8216;Attach existing policies directly<\/strong>&#8216;.<\/li><li>In the search bar, type &#8216;s3&#8217;. <\/li><li>Check mark on &#8216;<strong>AmazonS3FullAccess&#8217;<\/strong>.<\/li><li>Click &#8216;<strong>Next: Tags&#8217;<\/strong>.<\/li><\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/09\/User-Created-AWS-1.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2062\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\"><li>Skip to the last step and click on &#8216;<strong>Create User&#8217;<\/strong>.  You will then get the required <strong>Access keys and secret<\/strong>. <\/li><\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/09\/Access-Keys-AWS.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2067\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Copy these keys or download the CSV file to secure the key somewhere.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Creating Bucket<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You will now have to create a bucket to store the files coming from the Everest Forms. For this, please follow the steps below.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\"><li>On the search bar, type &#8216;<strong>S3<\/strong>&#8216; and click on the first option. <\/li><\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/09\/Bucket-AWS-Step1-1024x495.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2068\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\"><li>Click on &#8216;<strong>Create Bucket<\/strong>&#8216;.<\/li><\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/09\/Bucket-AWS-Step2-1024x421.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2069\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\"><li>Provide a name for the bucket.<\/li><li>Select the region. You will need to use the username and region later on so don&#8217;t forget them. <\/li><li>Leave everything else as they are and click on &#8216;<strong>Create Bucket&#8217;. <\/strong><\/li><\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/09\/Bucket-AWS-Step3-2-615x1024.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2070\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Integrating the Amazon S3 with Everest Forms<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Once you have generated the access keys and secret and also created a Bucket from your Amazon account, go to <strong>Everest Forms > Settings > Integration > Amazon S3<\/strong>. Add the <strong>AWS Access Key ID, Secret Key, AWS Region, and Amazon S3 Bucket Name<\/strong>. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">Note: <strong>AWS Region, and Amazon S3 Bucket Name<\/strong> should match with the one you used while creating the bucket.<\/pre>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/09\/AWS-EVF-Settings-1024x375.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2073\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\"><li>Click on &#8216;<strong>Authenticate with Amazon account<\/strong>&#8216;. <\/li><\/ul>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Uploading Files to the Amazon S3 via Everest Forms<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\"><li>Create a form or edit the existing one.<\/li><li>Add a File Upload folder. <\/li><li>Go to <strong>Form Settings &gt; General<\/strong>. <\/li><\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/09\/AWS-Individual-Form-Settings-1-1024x516.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2074\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\"><li>Select &#8216;<strong>Amazon S3 File path<\/strong>&#8216;. <\/li><li>Give a path.<\/li><li>If you want to send the form as a PDF to the Amazon S3, select &#8216;<strong>Send PDF To Amazon S3<\/strong>&#8216;.<\/li><\/ul>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">Note: You will have to install and activate the PDF Submission add-on for sending the PDF to Amazon S3.<\/pre>\n","protected":false},"doc_category":[48]},{"id":2895,"slug":"salesforce","link":"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/salesforce\/","title":{"rendered":"Salesforce"},"content":{"rendered":"\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Integrate the world&#8217;s leading CRM platform with one of the best form plugins in the game with the help of the Everest Forms-Salesforce add-on. The following documentation will let you know how to do this. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\" id=\"installation\">Installation<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Firstly, you need to purchase the Everest Forms Pro plugin.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>After the successful purchase, you will get the plugin zip file under your WPEverest account page.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Now, you can download the Everest forms pro plugin and <strong>Salesforce<\/strong> add-on from there.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Through your WordPress Dashboard, visit&nbsp;<strong>Plugins -&gt; Add New -&gt; Upload<\/strong>&nbsp;and then choose the downloaded zip file and then install and activate it.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Now you are ready to use this add-on for your site.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\"><strong>Alternative Method:<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<ol class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Once you have entered the Everest Forms Pro license on your site, go to <strong>Everest Forms &gt; Add-ons<\/strong>.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Search for the &#8216;<strong>Salesforce<\/strong>&#8216; add-on.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Click on &#8216;<strong>Install<\/strong>&#8216;.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Once the installation is finished, click on &#8216;<strong>Activate<\/strong>&#8216;. The add-on will be activated.<\/li>\n<\/ol>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Setup and Configuration<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You will need the Salesforce Consumer Key and Consumer Secret to integrate Salesforce with the Everest Forms. You'll encounter an error in the next step that looks like this if you try to move quickly:\n\n<strong>error=invalid_client_id&amp;error_description=client%20identifier%20invalid<\/strong>\n\nNot to worry! This message just indicates that Salesforce hasn't set up your connection. It's best to give yourself a little more time before trying again.<\/pre>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">If you get the following error in the URL or a 404 error,\n<strong>invalid_request\r\nmissing required code challenge<\/strong>\nTurn off \"Require Proof Key for Code Exchange (PKCE) Extension for Supported Authorization Flows\" checkbox in your Connected App's settings. Click on \u2018<strong>Authenticate with Salesforce Account\u2019.<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/08\/Salesforce-Integration-Setting-Page-1-3-1024x361.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2039\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">After clicking the above button, you will be asked to log in to your Salesforce account. Give a Salesforce Account Nickname. At last, click on &#8216;<strong>Connect to Salesforce<\/strong>&#8216; and you will be connected to the Salesforce account. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Adding Connections for individual forms<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Once you have set up the required credentials on the main settings, it\u2019s time for you to add a new connection for your forms. For this, go to the required form\u2019s editor page. Click on \u2018<strong>Integration\u2019&nbsp;<\/strong>from the available tabs. You will find an option called \u2018<strong>Salesforce<\/strong>\u2018 from which you can add a new connection.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Click on the \u2018<strong>Add New Connection<\/strong>\u2018 button and a pop-up will appear. Add the name of the connection.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/08\/Salesforce-Add-New-Connection-1024x427.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2044\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Click on &#8216;<strong>OK<\/strong>&#8216; after adding the name and the connection is added.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/09\/Salesforce-Mapping-Settings.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2099\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Now, select the required account, lists and then map the fields accordingly. Also, you can map the Everest Forms fields with the Salesforce&#8217;s custom fields.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h4 class=\"wp-block-heading\">How to use conditional logic for Salesforce connections?<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">To use conditional logic, enable the option by ticking the &#8216;<strong>Use Conditional Logic&#8217; <\/strong>option at the bottom of every connection you make.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"728\" height=\"173\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/06\/Conditional-Logic-HS.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1539\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/06\/Conditional-Logic-HS.png 728w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/06\/Conditional-Logic-HS-300x71.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/06\/Conditional-Logic-HS-360x86.png 360w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 728px) 100vw, 728px\" \/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">In the above example, the form sends data to the Salesforce account only if the First Name is &#8216;Everest&#8217;. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-preformatted\">Note: You can add more than one connections.<\/pre>\n","protected":false},"doc_category":[44]},{"id":2894,"slug":"how-to-add-a-custom-validation-message-for-the-required-field","link":"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/how-to-add-a-custom-validation-message-for-the-required-field\/","title":{"rendered":"How to add a custom validation message for the required field?"},"content":{"rendered":"\n<figure class=\"wp-block-embed is-type-video is-provider-youtube wp-block-embed-youtube wp-embed-aspect-16-9 wp-has-aspect-ratio\"><div class=\"wp-block-embed__wrapper\">\n<div class=\"zak-oembed-container\"><iframe loading=\"lazy\" title=\"Set Custom Validation Message for Required Fields in Everest Forms\" width=\"812\" height=\"457\" src=\"https:\/\/www.youtube.com\/embed\/Gs0-Menm2Ug?feature=oembed\" frameborder=\"0\" allow=\"accelerometer; autoplay; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture; web-share\" allowfullscreen><\/iframe><\/div>\n<\/div><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can add a common validation message for all the required fields in the available Everest Forms or, you can add a custom validation message for each individual form.  Both of these methods are described below.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Common\/Global Validation Message<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">If you want to set one message for all the required fields across the available forms, you can use this option. Go to <strong>Everest Forms &gt; Settings &gt; Validation.<\/strong> Now, add the message in the &#8216;<strong>Required<\/strong>&#8216; option&#8217;s input box.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/08\/Global-Validation-Message.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-2020\"\/><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Individual Required Field Validation Message<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">However, if you don&#8217;t want one message for all the form fields and want to add a custom validation message for each individual required form field, please go to the form builder. Click on the respective form field. to Configure the Phone Field with Extra Available Options? [Pro]"},"content":{"rendered":"\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The Phone number field in Everest Forms Pro allows user to add their contact phone numbers in the contact form. You can enhance user experience with smart validation, country restrictions, and input customization.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">To configure, go to the <strong>Field Options<\/strong> by clicking on the <strong>Phone Field<\/strong>. <span style=\"margin: 0px; padding: 0px;\">Yo<\/span>u can change the&nbsp;Label,&nbsp;Meta Key, and&nbsp;Description&nbsp;for the field from here. Also, you can see two formats for the phone number field: <strong>Default<\/strong> and <strong>Smart<\/strong>. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Default Format with Custom Input Mask<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">When you select the&nbsp;<strong>Format<\/strong>&nbsp;as&nbsp;<em>Default<\/em>, you can see the&nbsp;<strong>Input Mask<\/strong>&nbsp;option, where you can enter a custom input mask.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/02\/phone1-1024x391.png\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/02\/phone1-1024x391.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1179\"\/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">This option is best for&nbsp;<em>fixed formats<\/em>&nbsp;(e.g., US phone numbers) as you can manually define the pattern.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\"><strong>Example: Phone Number Input Mask<\/strong><\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">An input mask like&nbsp;<code>+1 (999) 999-9999<\/code>:<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>Automatically adds the&nbsp;<code>+1<\/code>&nbsp;country code and formats the number with parentheses and hyphens.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Restricts input to numbers only (the&nbsp;<code>9<\/code>&nbsp;symbol represents numeric digits).<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-code\"><code>Here's a detailed guide on <a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/docs\/how-to-use-custom-input-mask\/\">using custom input mask<\/a>. <\/code><\/pre>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Smart Format with Country Flag &amp; Validation<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">When you select the&nbsp;<strong>Format<\/strong>&nbsp;as&nbsp;<em>Smart<\/em>, it displays the country flag with validation in the dropdown so that users can select their country. Hence, it automatically validates the phone number based on the selected country. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/02\/phone2-1024x374.png\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/02\/phone2-1024x374.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1180\"\/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">The Smart format dynamically adjusts to the user\u2019s selected country, improving accuracy and usability. <strong>Save <\/strong>or <strong>Update <\/strong>the form to preview the form for phone field configuration. <\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/02\/phone1.png\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/02\/phone3.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-1181\"\/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<h2 class=\"wp-block-heading\">Limit Country Options in the Phone Number Field (New)<\/h2>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">By default, the <strong>Phone Number<\/strong> field in Everest Forms allows users to select a country from a full list of available options. However, if you want to restrict the dropdown to specific countries, use the filter <strong>everest_forms_smart_phone_allowed_countries<\/strong><\/p>\n\n\n\n<h3 class=\"wp-block-heading\">How to Restrict the Country List?<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">To limit the country list in the Phone Number field, add the following code snippet to your functions.php. You can use the <strong>Code Snippets<\/strong> plugin for this.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<pre class=\"wp-block-code\"><code>add_filter('everest_forms_smart_phone_allowed_countries', 'everest_forms_smart_phone_allowed_countries');\n\nfunction everest_forms_smart_phone_allowed_countries( $allowed_country )\n{\n&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; $allowed_country = array( 'US','CA', 'GB', 'NP');\n&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; return $allowed_country;\n}<\/code><\/pre>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li>You can replace &#8216;US&#8217;, &#8216;CA&#8217;, &#8216;GB&#8217; with the country codes of your choice.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>Users will only see the specified countries in the Phone Number field dropdown.<\/li>\n\n\n\n<li>If no filter is applied, all countries will be available by default.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/03\/add-code-snippet-to-filter-country.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1024\" height=\"419\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/03\/add-code-snippet-to-filter-country-1024x419.png\" alt=\"Add Code Snippet to Filter Country\" class=\"wp-image-4998\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/03\/add-code-snippet-to-filter-country-1024x419.png 1024w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/03\/add-code-snippet-to-filter-country-300x123.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/03\/add-code-snippet-to-filter-country-768x314.png 768w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/03\/add-code-snippet-to-filter-country-360x147.png 360w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2025\/03\/add-code-snippet-to-filter-country.png 1433w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 1024px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">You can find a complete list of country codes <a href=\"https:\/\/www.iban.com\/country-codes\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noreferrer noopener nofollow\">here<\/a> or refer to any international dialing code list. If left blank, the value defaults to the maximum size the server allows.<\/li><li><strong>Maximum number limit on uploads<\/strong>: This allows you to enter the number of image uploads for this field.<\/li><li><strong>Store image in WordPress Media Library<\/strong>:\u00a0By default, the uploaded images are stored at <strong>wp-content > uploads > everest_forms_upload<\/strong>. They are&nbsp;<em><strong>Single<\/strong><\/em>,&nbsp;<em><strong>Range<\/strong>,<\/em>&nbsp;and&nbsp;<em><strong>Multiple<\/strong><\/em>.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image Options.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/08\/Time-Field-2-1024x536.png\" alt=\"\" Options<\/h3>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">Also, there are the\u00a0<strong>Advanced Options,<\/strong>\u00a0where you can change more options for the Time field as well.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<ul class=\"wp-block-list\">\n<li><strong>Placeholder Text:<\/strong> You can add placeholder text to the Date\/Time field.<\/li>\n<\/ul>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-large\"><a Booking<\/h4>\n\n\n\n<p class=\"wp-block-paragraph\">In the Advanced Options, there is a setting called <strong>&#8216;Slot Booking&#8217;<\/strong>. This feature enables a booking form where users can select their desired booking time. Once a time is selected and the form is submitted, no other user will be able to choose the same time. If the time is already taken, an error message will appear, and the form will not be submitted.<\/p>\n\n\n\n<figure class=\"wp-block-image size-full\"><a href=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/08\/time-slot-error.png\"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"707\" height=\"487\" src=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/08\/time-slot-error.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"wp-image-4681\" srcset=\"https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/08\/time-slot-error.png 707w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/08\/time-slot-error-300x207.png 300w, https:\/\/docs.everestforms.net\/wp-content\/uploads\/2022\/08\/time-slot-error-360x248.png 360w\" sizes=\"auto, (max-width: 707px) 100vw, 707px\" \/><\/a><\/figure>\n","protected":false},"doc_category":[5]}]
